# Spec AB, orders 0–11 + the pre-measurement calibration gate: the Gen-2 widened-pool M2 learning-unit judgement with a pre-fixed effect-size criterion (implementation plan)

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Build every AB stage and run them in order:
- order 0 — code, tests, `bench`;
- 0a `stage0` (tests log, bench, the AA differential test, environment hash);
- 0b `reuse` (AB.2 reuse conditions and the `lv_odour` sources);
- 0c `generate` (the KC-pre set, every AB.3 declared value);
- 0d `seal_code`;
- 0e `cal_gate` (the pre-measurement calibration gate on g 5 · 6 · 7 and the synthetic validation);
- 3 `kc_input` (the first Gen-2 measurement);
- 4 `set`;
- 5 `smoke`;
- 6 `oracle` (the AB set is consumed from here on);
- 7 `screen`, then 7b `calibrate`;
- 8 `learn`;
- 9 `verdict`;
- 10 `records`.

Order 11 (AB.10, the spec 5 status line, README ko/en, p-latest, memory) is the controller's text work (Operator §6).

**Precedence:**
- The AB body (AB.0–AB.9.1, spec lines **6578–6922**) is the rule. AB.9.2 (**6924–7011**) lists what the red team changed and why.
- Where AB changes nothing, the AA body applies (AA.0–AA.9.2 with AA.9.3 folded in). Where AA changes nothing either, Y's order applies (Y.9.2 > Y.0–Y.9.1).
- The code boundary follows Z.9.2 P1-5 / AA.2 (AB.2 6648–6652).
- The brainstorm and the AB.9.2 scratch are sources, not rules (6580).

**What this plan does not do:**
- It never builds or measures H.5, the POOL odd turns (a), the 4.4 M4 set, or anything of W's main set (AB.9 6893).
- It never re-measures or rewrites V · W · X · Y · Z · AA blocks, raw files or caches.
- It has no G.6 operating characteristic, no BAND, no 2K re-measurement and no F.5 pair judgement (AB.1 개정 1–2, 6631–6632).
- It does not recompute G.6 (AB.9.1 해석 18).

**Architecture:**
- **New modules:**
  - `flymon/brain/ab_spec.py` (numbers);
  - `ab_store.py` (the only writer);
  - `ab_rules.py` (labels, AB.8 sentences, reasons, budget);
  - `ab_pairs.py` (the Gen-2 generator);
  - `ab_estimate.py` (statistics, TS / CG, calibration, verdict);
  - `ab_runner.py` (every stage);
  - `scripts/run_ab.py` (CLI).
- **New tests:**
  - `tests/brain/ab_world.py`;
  - `test_ab_spec.py`, `test_ab_store.py`, `test_ab_rules.py`, `test_ab_pairs.py`, `test_ab_estimate.py`, `test_ab_calibrate.py`;
  - `test_ab_runner_a.py` … `test_ab_runner_d.py`, `test_ab_cli.py`, `test_ab_static.py`;
  - `tests/brain/ab_mutants.py` (the scratch-copy mutant runner).
- **Modified:** `tests/brain/test_p_spec.py` (one line: `MODULES` gains `ab_spec`).
- **Imported, never edited** (AB.2 6648–6652; every name checked at plan time — table "Pre-validated imports" below): `aa_estimate`, `aa_rules`, `aa_store.ReadCache`, `aa_runner` (pure helpers only), `w_verdict`, `w_records`, `w_measure`, `w_runner.build_ctx` / `git_facts`, `w_pairs`, `v_pairs`, `t_pairs`, `e_pairs`, `l_pairs`, `h4_pairs`, `q_pairs`, `r_pairs`, `r_measure`, `u_measure.UPool`, `r_store`, `y_rules`, `y_runner.Runner._record` (a staticmethod), `h4_formula.pair_stats`, `v_runner.kc_values`, `e_rules.odour_activity` (through `v_records.kc_input_record`), `w_spec` / `v_spec` / `t_spec` / `y_spec`.
- **Never edited, never written:**
  - `flymon/brain/{aa,t,v,w,x,y,z}_*.py` and `scripts/run_{aa,w,x,y,z}.py`;
  - `results/{v,w,x,y,z,aa}/`;
  - `results/summary/{v_lever,w_learning,x_learning,y_learning,z_learning,aa_learning}.json`.

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.13, NumPy, SciPy (`stats.t`, `stats.beta`), poke_env 0.16.1 (`GenData`), pytest. Use `.venv/bin/python` everywhere. In the implementation worktree run Python with `PYTHONPATH=.`, because the venv's editable install points at the main checkout.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-14-flymon-design.md`, appendix **AB**, lines **6578–7011** at commit `5e3eb7e` (normative). The line numbers below refer to that commit. The AA plan (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-06-aa.md`) shows the reused patterns:
- the env hash, the chain and the guarded store;
- cells and the literal guard;
- the defect procedure and `restart_preseal`;
- the scratch-copy mutant runner (`tests/brain/aa_mutants.py`).

**Validated at plan time (2026-10-07, read-only on `5e3eb7e`; scratch worktree `ab-plan-scratch`, detached, removed before the plan commit):**
- **Code identical:** `git diff ea408a2 5e3eb7e -- flymon scripts tests` is empty. The AB declaration commit and HEAD have the same code (Reading 4).
- **Facts read from committed blocks, file metadata and keys only (no values):**
  - Every V / AA commit of AB.2 0b is an ancestor of HEAD.
  - `results/v/kc_input.json` sha256 = `f197492a…`.
  - All nine AA blocks are `PASS`. AA `candidates` holds 31 `trained` keys.
  - W `pilot.manifest` resolves to 16 pair keys, all among `r_pairs.even_rows`. Y `pilot.admission` has 7 keys: 3 among the even rows, 4 among the V set.
  - W / U measurement keys = `y_spec.SPEC.w_measure_key` / `u_measure_key`.
  - No integer in 87_400_001–102_000_000, and not 20261007, occurs in `flymon/`, `tests/` or `scripts/`.
- **Constants computed:**
  - c(7) = 1.1258688655, J(8) = 0.8888888889, δ = 1 / (J c) = 0.999228271, J × c = 1.000772.
  - CP thresholds: x ≤ 219 / 47 at n = 10 000, x ≤ 103 / 20 at n = 5 000 — exactly the AB.5 numbers.
  - The four stream test vectors (AB.2 6657) are pinned in `ab_spec.stream_vectors` (computed with `aa_estimate.stream`).
- **The scratch run.** The code blocks of Tasks 1, 3, 4, 5 and 6 were applied as written. `test_ab_spec`, `test_ab_store`, `test_ab_rules`, `test_ab_pairs` (real data), `test_ab_estimate`, `test_ab_calibrate` and `test_p_spec` give **85 passed, exit 0**. Facts from that run:
  - **Generator self-test:** the Gen-1 configuration through `ab_pairs.Walker` reproduces W's main set — (b) 167 · (a) 82, last turn 1967, digest `65dbf001…` — with W's own skip counts (cap 137, used 4 516, in_set 1 405, pool_only 1 099, kc_input 484).
  - **Every AB.3 declared value** is regenerated by `ab_pairs.generate` + `declared_reasons` → `[]`, including `gen1_same_keys` 23, the group tables and the four KC re-measurement odours. This holds **only with the declared-form tally of Reading 2** — see Risk 2.
  - **`lv_odour`:** 108 odours (AA31 45, all learned 71, V set 66), sorted-JSON sha256 `d8af2a63…`.
  - **AA differential:**
    - `ab_estimate.pair_stats` on AA's learn raw of S1's first two pairs (c 7, 32) equals `results/aa/records.json` bit for bit: the four gate means, the fly labels and the two raw contrasts.
    - `ab_estimate.ts_reps` + `ts_limits` at α 0.025 on AA's stream `(87_000_000, "pool", "S1")` equals AA `estimate.json`'s pooled two-stage CIs of both associations bit for bit.
  - **Pure-module mutants:** 30 concrete pure-module edits, applied on a **copy** under `/private/tmp`, each run against its killing test — killed **30 / 30** after the test fixes now in the blocks (Task 11 lists them).
  - **bench:** one replicate of the three statistics on g 7 with B = 10 000 takes ≈ 0.10 s single-process (cells 17 and 23). 24 cells × 1 000 reps on 16 spawn workers took 233 s wall (≈ 10.3 effective cores on this 6P + 12E machine).
- **Plan-time calibration probe** (synthetic only, streams `"probe"` / `"probe2"` under the cal root, results in `/private/tmp/claude-503/ab-plan-val/probe_cal*.log`, never a record):
  - g 7, all 24 cells × 1 000 reps;
  - then cells 19 / 21 × 3 000 reps on g 5 · 6 · 7.
  - The worst cell is (19), the left-skewed X-label effect. At the grid end α 0.0001 its joint PASS-side miss on g 7 is D 47/3 000 = 1.57 % and R 57/3 000 = 1.90 %; the first probe gave 21/1 000 and 25/1 000. AB.5 selection needs x ≤ 103 / 5 000 = 2.06 %, and verification needs x ≤ 219 / 10 000. **`STOP_CALIBRATION`〈0e〉 is therefore close to a coin flip** (Risk 1).
- **Not opened:** any per-pair value of Y's oracle, any 2nd-gen value (none exists), any AA value beyond the differential test's bit comparison (AA is closed; reading its raw is allowed).

## Global Constraints

- **Numbers live in `ab_spec.py`.** Any other `ab_*` file, or `scripts/run_ab.py`, that holds an int literal with |v| > 16, or a float literal other than 0.0 / 1.0, fails `test_ab_spec::test_no_ab_file_but_ab_spec_holds_a_number` (Z's guard, copied). Test files are not guarded.
- **HARD RULE — no value is read before its stage.**
  - Nobody opens `results/y/oracle.json`, `results/y/cache/r_oracle/`, or any 2nd-gen output under `results/ab/` (except the operator's exit-code / sentence lines).
  - The KC stage writes per-odour values: these are odour properties, allowed in its block (AB.7 3).
  - The oracle stage writes counts only to its block; per-pair oracle values go to `results/ab/oracle.json`, which nobody reads by hand (AB.7 6).
  - The learn block holds manifests only. The first learning statistic appears in `verdict`.
  - Implementers and reviewers never read real-run outputs. Tests use synthetic files in `tmp_path`.
- **AA is closed.** The AA differential test (Task 7) reads `results/aa/{learn,records,estimate}.json`, `results/aa/cache` (through `aa_store.ReadCache`) and `results/summary/aa_learning.json`, read-only, in `stage0` only.
- **Write boundary (AB.2 6651).**
  - AB writes only under `results/ab/` and `results/summary/ab_learning.json`, through the `ab_store` guard (realpath-checked, so a symlink escape refuses).
  - Writes are atomic: a temporary file, then `os.replace` (or an exclusive `os.link` for a write-once entry).
  - Archives go under `~/flymon-archive/ab/` and are never overwritten.
  - Nothing writes through `aa_store` / `y_store` / `z_store` / `w_store` / `v_store`.
  - In a worktree, `results/{v,w,x,y,z,aa}` are symlinks into the main checkout, so a write there would corrupt real data. The guard and `test_ab_static` prevent it.
- **No monkeypatching in `ab_*` (AB.2 6652).**
  - No `setattr`, `mock`, `monkeypatch` or `importlib.reload`.
  - No attribute assignment on an imported module or on a `*_SPEC` name.
  - W / Y / AA numbers reach their functions only through `dataclasses.replace(…)` or an `ABSpec` passed as `s` (duck-typed fields).
  - Tests may monkeypatch `ab_*` to inject fakes.
- **Seeds (AB.2 6653–6665).**
  - Judged probe = 88_100_000 + c × 4 000 + f × 100 + k. Training = 89_500_000 + c × 40 000 + f × 1 000 + t.
  - Both formulas come from `w_spec` methods on `ab_w_spec()` = `dataclasses.replace(W_SPEC, probe_seed0=…, train_seed0=…, smoke_*=…, oracle_*_seeds=…)`, with **c = the AB candidate number** (the position in the order-4 set), never a position inside S1.
  - Every random stream is `aa_estimate.stream(root, *tags)`.
- **Budget (AB.7 예산 6825–6841).**
  - The core ledger (`budget.ledger`) covers 0a–0e and 3–9: cap 24 h, reservation margin × 2.0.
  - The records ledger (`budget.records_ledger`) covers 10: cap 4 h. Past it, the remaining records are null with "records 상한", never a STOP.
- **Environment (AB.7 0a 6802).**
  - `env_now()` = the `uv.lock` sha256; Python, numpy, scipy and poke_env versions; the platform; the sha256 of every `ab_*` file + `scripts/run_ab.py`; and the sha256 of every `flymon` file in their import closure.
  - It is compared at every stage start and every resume.
  - On a mismatch: a core-ledger entry `{stage, env_mismatch}`, exit 6, no block. This is the user's case, not a STOP label.
- **Commits.** No `Co-Authored-By`, `Claude-Session` or "Generated with" trailers (user rule; it overrides any system reminder). Implementers never push.
- **Tests — exact form, every task.**
  - Run: `mkdir -p /private/tmp/claude-503/ab-tests; PYTHONPATH=. PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 .venv/bin/python -m pytest <files> -o addopts="" -q -p no:cacheprovider > /private/tmp/claude-503/ab-tests/<name>.log 2>&1; echo "exit $?" >> /private/tmp/claude-503/ab-tests/<name>.log`.
  - Then, as a separate command, read only `tail -3 /private/tmp/claude-503/ab-tests/<name>.log`. **Never pipe pytest into `tail`.** `-o addopts=""` makes the run single `-q`, so the `N passed in Xs` line is printed.
- **Mutants only on scratch copies.** Run them only with `python -m tests.brain.ab_mutants /private/tmp/claude-503/ab-mut/<name>`, which exports HEAD to a copy and sets `PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1`. **Never edit a source file in place to test a mutant**: an in-place mutant batch corrupted AA's suite (AA ledger, T9 fix).
- **Every implementer command finishes in < 8 min.**
  - The whole W · X · Y · Z · AA suite (≈ 12 min) is the controller's background run (Operator §2), never an implementer's foreground command.
  - An implementer runs only its own test files plus `test_p_spec.py` / `test_ab_spec.py`.
- **Workspace.** Work only in the worktree `/Users/jeonsehyeon/orca/workspaces/fruit-fly/guillemot-ab` (branch `ab-impl`, created by the controller — Operator §0). The main checkout is touched only in the Operator section.

## Review Focus

1. **0c reproduces AB.3 exactly — with the declared-form tally.**
   - Expected: `ab_pairs.declared_reasons(generate(…)) == []` on real data.
   - The in-order counts are recorded beside it: `in_set` 845, `lv_odour` 1 276 (Reading 2).
   - The Gen-1 self-test gives digest `65dbf001…`.
   - Owner: Task 4 (`test_generate_matches_every_declared_value`, `test_selftest_reproduces_w_main_set`).
2. **0e is a gate, not a record.**
   - Expected: any structure × statistic whose PASS-side α is not verified → `STOP_CALIBRATION`〈0e〉, exit 3, with no Gen-2 measurement.
   - The checkpoints in `results/ab/cal/` resume bit for bit after a kill.
   - The measured cap stops the gate with `STOP_BUDGET`〈0e〉 and keeps the checkpoints.
   - Owner: Task 8 (`test_cal_gate_stop_calibration`, `test_cal_gate_resumes_checkpoints_bit_equal`, `test_cal_gate_measured_cap`).
3. **The verdict is IUT over 4 gates × 3 statistics × 2 methods with the 7b levels.**
   - FAIL is Bonferroni at α_F on both methods. FAIL is impossible when α_F is unreached. Comparisons are rounded to 9 digits and strict.
   - Owner: Tasks 5 and 10 (fixtures (i)–(xii), `test_verdict_reads_7b_levels`).
4. **Consumption and states.**
   - The oracle block holds counts only and marks `oracled`. The screen marks `screened`.
   - Learn marks `training` at round start and `trained` only after every planned unit key is cached with its sha256 in the manifest.
   - A killed learn resumes from the cache with no `STOP`. A cache-hit result equals a fresh one bit for bit.
   - Owner: Tasks 9 and 10.
5. **The AA differential test (stage0).** Bit equality of unit keys, counts, gate stats, pair estimates and the TS CI against AA's committed raw. A cache miss is a failure, never a measurement. Owner: Task 7.
6. **No value before its stage; nothing written outside `results/ab/`.** Owner: Task 11 (`test_full_chain_leaves_others_untouched`, `test_learn_block_has_no_statistic`, the static AST check).

## Readings (decisions this plan makes where the spec leaves a choice; disclose in AB.10)

1. **Stage names.** AB.7 6779 gives the order. The stages are:
   - `stage0` (0a) · `reuse` (0b) · `generate` (0c) · `seal_code` (0d) · `cal_gate` (0e);
   - `kc_input` (3) · `set` (4) · `smoke` (5) · `oracle` (6) · `screen` (7) · `calibrate` (7b) · `learn` (8) · `verdict` (9) · `records` (10).

   Order 0 is this plan's code and tests. `bench` (AB.7 0 6799) runs inside `stage0` and is recorded in its block, as 6802 says. Order 11 is controller text.
2. **The AB.3 declared skip counts are the declared-form tally (6674–6684, 6671 "건너뛴 행의 키는 taken에 넣지 않는다 … 결과는 같다").**
   - The in-order walk with `lv_odour` gives `in_set` 845 and `lv_odour` 1 276. A key skipped by `lv_odour` is skipped again at each repeat, instead of counting `in_set`.
   - The table's numbers are those of the walk **without** `lv_odour` (`used` 2 765 · `in_set` 1 994 · `pool_only` 242 · `cap` 138), followed by the rows of that walk that carry an lv odour ((a) 9 · (b) 118).
   - The rows are identical either way, exactly as 6672's last sentence says. That sentence is the spec's own reason why the two views agree, so the plan compares the declared form and records the in-order counter in the same block.
   - Validated: `declared_reasons == []`. This is Risk 2.
3. **The opponent type-set group key** (AB.5 6726, 해석 3) is `"+".join(sorted(opp_x))`. Groups are numbered by first appearance in c order. The X-label groups are `y_rules.merge_groups` over the row keys.
4. **"이 부록 커밋 시점" (AB.2 6647)** = `ea408a2`. The sha256 of every `t_* · v_* · w_* · y_* · aa_*.py` and of `z_split.py` at `ea408a2` (`git show ea408a2:<file>`) must equal the working tree. `git diff ea408a2 5e3eb7e -- flymon scripts tests` is empty.
5. **Two z's (AB.2 6642, 6645).**
   - The oracle uses `W_SPEC.z_v()`: 3-decimal z_V, the Y 0p call and the cache key.
   - The naive screen, learn, verdict and records use W block `reuse`'s full-precision z_V: AA's path, read from `results/summary/w_learning.json`.
   - `smoke` checks both against their sources.
6. **D_fin's TS (AB.4 6703, AB.5 6724–6725).**
   - The four gates share one draw on the full S1 X-label group list (6705 "같은 재표집 색인을 공유한다"). A ±∞ fly is NaN.
   - A pair without a finite fly in a gate is all-NaN in that gate. It drops out of every replicate by `_nanmean` (AB.2 6649 "±∞ 마리를 NaN으로 두고 `_nanmean`"), and the limits are `numpy.nanpercentile`.
   - The CG and the k_fin / g_fin floors use the remaining pairs and their groups (6725 "D_fin에서는 남은 쌍과 그 묶음으로").
   - The calibration cells never produce such a pair, so the two readings coincide there.
7. **Streams** (AB.2 6657–6660; no new root).

   | Stream | Tags |
   |---|---|
   | judgement TS | `("pool", "S1")`, `("pool_drop", "S1")`, `("pool_raw", "S1")` |
   | records CIs | `("pool_fly", "S1")`, `("pool_pair", "S1")`, `("naive", "S1")`, `("pair", c)` |
   | floor sensitivity | `("sens_floor", "sens")`, and its calibration structure tag `"sens"` under the cal root |
   | calibration | `("cal", tag, "sel" \| "truth" \| "ver", ci)` with ci = 1…24, the spec's 1-based cell numbers (the test vectors use 1, 23 and 24) |
   | bench | `("bench", ci)` under the cal root (never a calibration count) |
   | synthetic validation | `("synth", config, ci)` under 88_030_000 |

   Every stream comes from `aa_estimate.stream`.
8. **The calibration truth (AB.5 6733)** is the superpopulation mean.
   - Each of the 10^6 simulated pairs draws its own effect from the cell's marginal: N(0, SD); the sum of two independent draws for (14–17); the skew form for (18–21); a per-fly N(0, 1) for (22); two constant flies for (23 / 24).
   - The draws come in chunks of `truth_chunk` = 50 000.
   - D_fin's truth averages the pairs with ≥ 1 finite fly.
   - The structure only names the stream, so the truth of a cell is the same in expectation for every structure.
9. **Calibration chunks and checkpoints (AB.5 6760).**
   - A (structure, phase, cell) job runs its reps in chunks of `cal_chunk` = 250 on its one stream.
   - After every chunk it atomically writes `results/ab/cal/<tag>/<phase>_<ci>.json` = {key, done, n, counts, state (PCG64), truth, wall_s}. The key is the sha256 of (tag, phase, ci, structure, truth, `only`, seal key).
   - A resumed job restores the PCG64 state and continues. The result is bit-identical (`test_cal_run_reproducible_resumable_and_monotone`).
   - Verification jobs count only the chosen α of each statistic and side (`only=`), so the verification stream structurally cannot inform the selection (6750 "검증 흐름을 선택에 쓰지 않는다").
10. **The representative structures and the synthetic validation (AB.5 6752, 6761–6765).**
    - The structures are `rep_structure(sizes)`: pairs in group order, type sets by pair number mod 9.
    - "(1) 한 관문 δ = 0.999228, 나머지 세 관문 δ = 3.0" is run once, with reward_level at the bar. By the mirror argument (해석 21) every gate gives the same distribution.
    - So there are 5 configurations (bar_one, bar_all, eff1.5, eff2.5, eff3.5) × cells (1), (4), (12), (19) × 1 000 reps.
    - Each gate's contrasts are generated independently and multiplied by `SIGNS[g]`, so every gate's expected direction holds. The levels are g 7's verified α (α_F only when reached).
    - The synthetic validation runs only when g 7's three PASS-side α are verified. Otherwise it is null with the reason.
11. **Comparisons.** The verdict rounds to 9 digits, strictly (AB.4 6711, 해석 14). The calibration miss comparisons are unrounded: a continuous statistic against a continuous truth.
12. **FAIL side unreached (AB.4 6714, AB.5 6757).** With α_F = None the FAIL row is never evaluated. Every UNDECIDED then carries the cause "FAIL 쪽 보정 미도달", because the FAIL evaluation was impossible for that set.
13. **The pooled naive d′ record (AB.4 6720).** Per fly, the ±10-winsorized `w_verdict.dprime` of the naive pre ΔV over its K probes. Pair = the fly mean, point = the pair mean, CI = the two-stage 2.5 / 97.5 % on `("naive", "S1")`.
14. **"관문별 마리 몫 |d′| ≥ 1"** (AB.4 6720, AB.8 6869) uses the uncorrected fly d′ (F.5's fly bar) times the gate's sign: round(σ d′ − 1, 9) ≥ 0. "±10 마리" = |d′| > 10, ±∞ included (AA's `n_clipped`). "그 가운데 ±∞" = the ±∞ flies.
15. **`restart_preseal`** (AB.7 0d 6806, 해석 28).
    - Allowed while no `kc_input` block exists (in the working or the HEAD summary) and `results/ab/cache/` is empty. That covers both pre-seal code fixes and post-seal / pre-measurement defects. The env hash makes a code fix after any committed block refuse every later stage, so both need the restart.
    - `--note <json>` = {symptom, clause, cause}.
    - It writes block `restart_preseal_<n>` = {symptom, clause, cause, old seal key or null, `invalidated` = the copied earlier blocks, each marked `INVALID`}.
    - It moves `results/ab` → `results/ab.invalid-<n>` and `~/flymon-archive/ab` → `…ab.invalid-<n>`; nothing is deleted.
    - It rewrites the summary to {`restart_preseal_<n>` blocks, `budget`}: the core ledger is carried, so spent time still counts.
    - The operator commits it, reruns §2, then runs from `stage0`.
16. **The defect procedure after the first measurement (AB.7 0d 6807; AA.7 3).**
    - `defect_<n>` → patch + regression test → §2 → `reseal_<n>` (new tests log, env rebased on `ab_files` only) → `recompute <stage>`.
    - `recompute` is allowed for `set`, `oracle`, `screen`, `calibrate`, `learn`, `verdict` and `records`. It runs cache hits only (a miss refuses with exit 2) and writes `<stage>_v<n>`. The replaced block goes into `invalid`.
17. **Candidate states (AB.3 6691, AB.7 8 6819).**
    - Inside a running stage, states live in `results/ab/progress/<stage>.candidates.json`.
    - `oracled` is set at the oracle batch start, and `screened` at the screen batch start (conservative: a killed run's pairs count as seen).
    - `training` is set at a learn round start. `trained` is set only when every planned (fly × brain) unit key of that pair is in the cache and its sha256 is in the stage manifest.
    - The tracked `candidates` block is rewritten from the progress file whenever any block is written.
18. **Budget estimates (AB.7 0e 6809, 5 6812, 7b 6817, 8 6818).**
    - **Before smoke** (the 0e reservation): the AB.7 table's prior unit costs (`ab_spec.prior_*`):
      - KC = 75 odours × 756.9 / 173 s;
      - oracle = 184 × 11 039 / 249 s;
      - screen = 40 × 1 034 / 31 s;
      - learn = 26 × 18 505 / 31 s;
      - fixed 0.5 h; verdict 0.1 h.
    - **0e and 7b** = cells × (n_sel + n_ver) × reps cost / workers.
      - Before 0e, the reps cost is `bench.max_s` (g 7; conservative for g 5 / 6), × 26 / 25 for the k = 26 structure of 7b.
      - At smoke and later, the reps cost is 0e's measured wall per rep × workers.
    - **At smoke:** the smoke job walls, with W's round arithmetic:
      - oracle = ⌈n_set / workers⌉ × the oracle job wall;
      - screen = ⌈40 × 8 / workers⌉ × the naive job wall;
      - learn = ⌈26 × 8 × 3 / workers⌉ × the largest learn job wall + noplast (⌈4 / workers⌉ × the same wall);
      - verdict = one timed synthetic verdict at k 26.
    - **7b-before and 8-before** use the real k. The measured cap is checked before each learn round and between 0e cells.
19. **The learn measured cap (AB.7 8 6818)** is checked only before each measurer round (AA Reading 28). A last round that crosses 24 h still ends `PASS`.
20. **The smoke row** b|17 is the V-set row with its E-grid odours (`ab_pairs.attach` = V's codebook / dual rule, = V `jm:L`'s row).
    - The smoke cache is `results/ab/smoke/cache`, with the smoke seed set = probe 88_040_000 + f × 100 + k (f < 32, k < 100) ∪ the smoke oracle seeds 88_060_000 + {0–7, 100–107, 200–207}.
    - The smoke blocks are 88_040_000–88_049_999 (probe), 88_050_000–88_059_999 (training) and 88_060_000–88_069_999 (oracle).
21. **noplast (AB.7 8 6818, 해석 15).**
    - S1's first two pairs in c order × 2 flies, R brain, `plastic=False`, relabelled `"noplast"`, the judged / training seeds of their c, block `"noplast"`.
    - It is measured in the learn stage after the S1 rounds.
    - R1 ≠ pre (bit) → `STOP_MACHINE`〈8〉.
22. **The verdict's second computation (AB.7 9 6821).** The same raw and code, computed again. Not bit-equal → `INVALID` (exit 5), never committed, then the defect procedure.
23. **The KC stage (AB.7 3 6810).**
    - One `RMeasurer.activity` call per engine on the 71 new + 4 repro odours (block `"kc_input"`, V's seeds and strength, the W measurement key's cache).
    - The block holds the new odours' two medians and the repro comparison.
    - Order 4 merges V's cache values and the new medians (`ab_pairs.kc_merge`).
24. **The oracle detail** `results/ab/oracle.json` holds every pair's Y 0p record (`y_runner.Runner._record`) and the manifest.
    - The block holds counts only: by axis testable, N_len, and the X-label / type-set group counts of the lenient pairs.
    - The lenient test is `y_rules.passes(rec, Y_SPEC)["y_lenient"]`.
25. **The screen re-read.** As AA Reading 7, the screen raw is re-read by manifest (sha-checked) and its digest recomputed right after the screen block and again at the start of `calibrate`. A difference → `STOP_MACHINE`〈7〉.
26. **0b writes the `lv_odour` list and its sha256 into the `reuse` block.** 0c reads the list from that block, never recomputing it (AB.2 6647 "생성 단계 0c는 그 목록만 쓴다").
27. **The skew shape** follows the AB.5 text: u = ±2 × (e^Z − e^{1/2}) / √(e(e − 1)), SD 2.
    - The AB.9.2 scratch (`sim_cal.py`: `a * SKEW(z)` with a = 2.0, and `SKEW` already × 2) used SD 4 for these cells, so its quoted skew-cell numbers are not those of the normative model.
    - The plan-time probe shows the normative SD 2 cell (19) still at the selection threshold (Risk 1).
    - The body rules (6580 "이 부록이 맞다").
28. **X labels depend on the permutation.** AB.3 6687 says the key *set* does not depend on the shuffle, and that holds. But a row's X label (the move *name*) is the first-taken occurrence of its key. A different seed changes the X-label group table: 20261006 gives Earthquake 42 · Surf 29 · …. With the declared seed the table matches. This is disclosed only.
29. **Exit codes:** 0 PASS · 2 refusal · 3 STOP · 5 INVALID · 6 env mismatch · 7 seal mismatch (AA's).
30. **The futility gate 0f (AB.9.3, user-directed, announced during planning).** It is planned as its own order between 0e and 3 (Tasks 6b, 8; Operator §0b), with every constant `TODO(AB.9.3)`. Where AB.9.3's text differs from this plan's sketch, AB.9.3 rules. A `STOP_FUTILE` is a pre-measurement STOP: the closure rule applies and the AB set is unused.
31. **The candidate ledger before order 4** is empty: the AB set does not exist yet (AB.3 6691 "AB 세트 각 후보"). The STOP tails of 0b–3 therefore read all-zero counts.

## File structure

| File | Responsibility | Task |
|---|---|---|
| `flymon/brain/ab_spec.py` | every AB number, seeds, stream vectors, cells, grids, budget priors, paths, sealed subset | 1 |
| `tests/brain/test_p_spec.py` | `MODULES` += `ab_spec` | 1 |
| `flymon/brain/ab_store.py` | guarded atomic writer, two ledgers, `candidates`, `ABCache`, `ReadCache`, archive, restart helpers | 2 |
| `flymon/brain/ab_rules.py` | labels, AB.8 sentences, tail + closure, reuse / KC / oracle reasons, budget arithmetic | 3 |
| `flymon/brain/ab_pairs.py` | Gen-2 opponents, `Walker`, self-test, `lv_sources`, `generate`, `declared_reasons`, `ab_set`, `set_record`, `attach`, `new_odour_inputs` | 4 |
| `flymon/brain/ab_estimate.py` | statistics, TS / CG, `set_limits`, `verdict`, record helpers (Task 5); calibration, `calibrate` / `calibrate_many`, `bench`, synthetic validation (Task 6); futility simulation (Task 6b, AB.9.3) | 5, 6, 6b |
| `flymon/brain/ab_runner.py` | env, closure, seal, ctx, chain, cells, every stage, restart / defect / reseal / recompute | 7–10 |
| `scripts/run_ab.py` | CLI | 7 |
| `tests/brain/ab_world.py` | synthetic world (fake ctx; w_world's Model / FakePool behind the real WMeasurer; scripted oracle / KC measurers; fake AA files) | 7 |
| `tests/brain/ab_mutants.py`, `tests/brain/test_ab_static.py` | mutant catalogue (scratch-copy runner) and static / chain checks | 11 |

**Task graph and parallel groups.**
- **P0:** T1.
- **P1:** {T2 ‖ T3 ‖ T4 ‖ T5}, after T1. They own disjoint files.
- **P2:** T6 → T6b, after T5 (same file; T6b needs the committed AB.9.3, Operator §0b).
- **P3:** T7, after T2, T3, T4 and T6.
- **P4:** T8 (also after T6b) → T9 → T10, sequential (same file `ab_runner.py`).
- **P5:** T11, after T10.

Twelve tasks (T1–T11 with T6b): the SDD loop (implementer → reviewer → ledger) applies. P1 is the only parallel group.

---
### Task 1: `ab_spec` — every AB number

**Parallel group:** P0 (first). **Depends on:** nothing.

**Files:**
- Create: `flymon/brain/ab_spec.py`, `tests/brain/test_ab_spec.py`
- Modify: `tests/brain/test_p_spec.py:31` (`MODULES` += `ab_spec`)

**Spec:**
- AB.2 6642–6665: engine, keys, KC, oracle seeds, reuse facts, seed blocks, stream vectors.
- AB.3 6667–6692: opponents 73 · 27, 15 / 9 new type sets, 1 168 combos, seed 20261007, skip order, the declared table, the group tables, the KC repro odours (6810).
- AB.4 6706–6719: J(8) 0.8889, c(7) 1.1259, bar ±1, R ±0.25 z, k_min 8, g_min 5, rounding 9.
- AB.5 6724–6765: B 10 000; the P / F grids and targets 0.025 / 0.00625; CP 97.5 %; 24 cells; n_sel 5 000 / n_ver 10 000; truth 10^6; δ 0.999228; representative structures g 5 / 6 / 7; synthetic cells (1), (4), (12), (19) × 1 000; bench cells (17), (23) × 200.
- AB.6 6773 (0.10); AB.7 예산 6826–6841 (24 h · 4 h · × 2.0 · k 26 · lenient 40 · the prior unit costs).

**Interfaces — produces:** `ab_spec.SPEC: ABSpec` (frozen) and `small(spec, **kw)`, with:
- `seal_fields()`, `stage_label(stage)`, `sealed_stages()`;
- `oracle_seeds()`, `smoke_oracle_seeds()`;
- `root(name)` ("boot" / "cal" / "synth");
- `cell(ci)` (1-based), `rep_sizes(tag)`.

Later tasks read only these names.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing test** `tests/brain/test_ab_spec.py`:

```python
"""AB's numbers (AB.2–AB.9.1): the declared values, AB's seed blocks colliding with nothing declared, the stream test
vectors, the sealed subset, and no AB file but ab_spec holding a number (Z's literal guard)."""
import ast
import dataclasses
import importlib
import math
from pathlib import Path

from flymon.brain import d6a
from flymon.brain.ab_spec import SPEC, ABSpec
from flymon.brain.v_spec import SPEC as V
from flymon.brain.w_spec import SPEC as W
from flymon.brain.y_spec import SPEC as Y
from tests.brain.test_p_spec import MODULES, _collect

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


def test_ab_spec_is_enumerated_for_the_collectors():
    assert MODULES.get("flymon/brain/ab_spec.py") == "flymon.brain.ab_spec"


def test_chain():
    assert SPEC.stages == ("stage0", "reuse", "generate", "seal_code", "cal_gate", "kc_input", "set", "smoke",
                           "oracle", "screen", "calibrate", "learn", "verdict", "records")
    lab = dict(SPEC.stage_labels)
    assert [lab[s] for s in SPEC.stages] == ["0a", "0b", "0c", "0d", "0e", "3", "4", "5", "6", "7", "7b", "8", "9", "10"]
    assert SPEC.sealed_stages()[0] == "cal_gate" and SPEC.first_measure == "kc_input"


def test_reuse_and_generator_facts():
    assert SPEC.kc_input_sha256.startswith("f197492a") and SPEC.w_digest_keys == Y.main_digest_keys
    assert (SPEC.w_n_b, SPEC.w_n_a, SPEC.w_last_turn) == (Y.main_n_b, Y.main_n_a, Y.main_last_turn)
    assert [c for _, c in SPEC.v_commits] == ["928eaad", "7dc199d", "cf0b3b2", "a279a56", "69f7b2d"]
    assert len(SPEC.aa_blocks) == 9 and SPEC.decl_commit == "ea408a2"
    assert (SPEC.n_opp, SPEC.n_combos, SPEC.shuffle_seed) == (73, 1168, 20261007)
    assert sum(dict(SPEC.decl_pre_kc).values()) == 209 and sum(dict(SPEC.decl_post_kc_max).values()) == 184
    assert sum(dict(SPEC.decl_lv).values()) == 127 and dict(SPEC.decl_odours) == {"all": 107, "v_cache": 36, "new": 71}
    assert sum(v for _, v in SPEC.decl_a_x_groups) == 139 and sum(v for _, v in SPEC.decl_a_t_groups) == 139
    assert len(SPEC.kc_repro) == 4 and SPEC.lv_odour_n == 108


def test_criterion_and_calibration_numbers():
    nu = SPEC.probes - 1
    c7 = math.sqrt(nu / 2) * math.gamma((nu - 1) / 2) / math.gamma(nu / 2)
    j = 1 - 3 / (4 * nu - 1)
    assert round(c7, 4) == SPEC.c7_declared and round(j, 4) == SPEC.hedges_j_declared
    assert round(1 / (j * c7), 6) == SPEC.delta_declared
    assert (SPEC.bar, SPEC.raw_min, SPEC.k_min, SPEC.g_min, SPEC.winsor) == (1.0, 0.25, 8, 5, 10.0)
    assert SPEC.p_grid == (0.025, 0.0125, 0.005, 0.0025, 0.001, 0.0005, 0.00025, 0.0001)
    assert SPEC.f_grid == (0.00625, 0.0025, 0.001, 0.0005, 0.00025, 0.0001, 0.00005, 0.00002, 0.00001)
    assert (SPEC.p_target, SPEC.f_target, SPEC.f_target * 4) == (0.025, 0.00625, 0.025)
    assert (SPEC.n_sel, SPEC.n_ver, SPEC.truth_pairs, SPEC.boot_b) == (5_000, 10_000, 1_000_000, 10_000)
    assert len(SPEC.cells) == 24 and SPEC.synth_cells == (1, 4, 12, 19) and SPEC.bench_cells == (17, 23)
    assert (SPEC.floor_const, SPEC.floor_flies, SPEC.skew_sd) == (0.05, 2, 2.0)
    assert (SPEC.core_cap_h, SPEC.records_cap_h, SPEC.cost_margin, SPEC.smoke_k, SPEC.smoke_lenient) == (24.0, 4.0, 2.0, 26, 40)


def test_ab_seed_blocks_collide_with_nothing_declared():
    out, seen = set(), set()
    _collect(SPEC, out, seen)
    roots = {SPEC.oracle_act_seed0, SPEC.oracle_select_seed0, SPEC.oracle_report_seed0, SPEC.boot_seed, SPEC.cal_seed,
             SPEC.synth_seed, SPEC.smoke_probe_seed0, SPEC.smoke_train_seed0, SPEC.smoke_oracle_seed0,
             SPEC.probe_seed0, SPEC.train_seed0}
    assert roots <= out and all(any(lo <= r < hi for lo, hi in SPEC.seed_blocks) for r in roots)
    for (a, b), (c, d) in zip(SPEC.seed_blocks, SPEC.seed_blocks[1:]):
        assert b <= c
    # the judged probe / training formulas stay in their blocks for c < 300
    assert SPEC.probe_seed0 + 299 * W.cand_probe_stride + 7 * W.fly_probe_stride + 7 < 89_300_000
    assert SPEC.train_seed0 + 299 * W.cand_train_stride + 7 * W.fly_train_stride + 39 < 101_500_000
    declared, seen = set(d6a.SEEDS), set()
    for path, mod in MODULES.items():
        if path == "flymon/brain/ab_spec.py":
            continue
        m = importlib.import_module(mod)
        _collect(m.SPEC, declared, seen)
        if hasattr(m, "smoke"):
            _collect(m.smoke(m.SPEC), declared, seen)
    assert not any(lo <= x < hi for x in declared for lo, hi in SPEC.seed_blocks)
    assert set(V.kc_seeds()) == set(range(24_002_000, 24_002_008))       # reused on purpose (AB.2)
    assert not any(dataclasses.is_dataclass(getattr(SPEC, f.name)) for f in dataclasses.fields(ABSpec))


def test_oracle_seed_lists():
    o = SPEC.oracle_seeds()
    assert o["act"][0] == 88_000_000 and o["select"][-1] == 88_000_107 and o["report"][0] == 88_000_200
    assert SPEC.smoke_oracle_seeds()["report"][-1] == 88_060_207


def test_seal_fields():
    f = SPEC.seal_fields()
    for k in ("bar", "raw_min", "k_min", "g_min", "p_grid", "f_grid", "cells", "n_sel", "n_ver", "stream_vectors",
              "rep_structures", "boot_b", "delta_declared"):
        assert k in f, k


def _numbers(path):
    out = set()
    for n in ast.walk(ast.parse(path.read_text())):
        if isinstance(n, ast.Constant) and type(n.value) in (int, float):
            out.add(n.value)
    return out


def test_no_ab_file_but_ab_spec_holds_a_number():
    files = [p for p in sorted((ROOT / "flymon/brain").glob("ab_*.py")) if p.name != "ab_spec.py"]
    if (ROOT / "scripts/run_ab.py").exists():
        files.append(ROOT / "scripts/run_ab.py")
    for p in files:
        bad = {v for v in _numbers(p) if (type(v) is int and abs(v) > 16) or (type(v) is float and v not in (0.0, 1.0))}
        assert not bad, (p.name, bad)
```

```python
"""Every number of spec appendix AB (lines 6578–7011; the body AB.0–AB.9.1 is the rule, AB.9.2 lists the red-team
changes). Y's filter numbers and V's KC numbers are read from y_spec / v_spec and never restated; the facts AB checks
against committed blocks (commits, digests, sha256, counts) are restated here because AB.2 / AB.3 declare them.
- Seeds (AB.2): AB's own roots carry "seed" in their field names so the collectors in test_p_spec see them; V's KC
  strength seeds (24_002_000 + i) are reused on purpose and come from v_spec.SPEC.kc_seeds().
- Streams (AB.2 "흐름 만들기"): every stream is aa_estimate.stream(root, *tags); the four test vectors are sealed.
- seal_fields(): the numbers the seal (AB.7 0d) hashes; seal_files: the AB rule files and every flymon file they
  import (test_ab_spec checks the import closure)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
from dataclasses import dataclass

SD4 = (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ABSpec:
    # ---- the chain (AB.7 순서 요약; plan Reading 1) --------------------------------------------------------------
    stages: tuple = ("stage0", "reuse", "generate", "seal_code", "cal_gate", "kc_input", "set", "smoke", "oracle",

                         "hedges_j_declared", "c7_declared", "delta_declared", "boot_b", "boot_chunk", "p_grid",
                         "f_grid", "p_target", "f_target", "cp_level", "n_sel", "n_ver", "truth_pairs", "truth_chunk",
                         "cal_chunk", "cells", "skew_sd", "floor_const", "floor_flies", "n_type_sets",
                         "rep_structures", "synth_struct", "synth_cells", "synth_reps", "synth_effects", "synth_other",
                         "boot_seed", "cal_seed", "synth_seed", "probe_seed0", "train_seed0", "stream_vectors",
                         "floor_cut", "compare_refs", "noplast_pairs", "noplast_flies", "shuffle_seed", "pct", "pct_scale")

    def seal_fields(self) -> dict:
        return {k: getattr(self, k) for k in self.seal_names}

    def stage_label(self, stage: str) -> str:
        return dict(self.stage_labels).get(stage, stage)

    def sealed_stages(self) -> tuple:
        return self.stages[self.stages.index("seal_code") + 1:]

    def oracle_seeds(self) -> dict:
        n = self.n_oracle_seeds
        return dict(act=list(range(self.oracle_act_seed0, self.oracle_act_seed0 + n)),
                    select=list(range(self.oracle_select_seed0, self.oracle_select_seed0 + n)),
                    report=list(range(self.oracle_report_seed0, self.oracle_report_seed0 + n)))

    def smoke_oracle_seeds(self) -> dict:
        o, n = self.smoke_oracle_seed0, self.n_oracle_seeds
        return dict(act=list(range(o, o + n)), select=list(range(o + 100, o + 100 + n)),
                    report=list(range(o + 200, o + 200 + n)))

    def root(self, name: str) -> int:
        return dict(boot=self.boot_seed, cal=self.cal_seed, synth=self.synth_seed)[name]

    def cell(self, ci: int) -> tuple:
        """Cell (ci) of AB.5, 1-based (the spec's (1)–(24))."""
        if not 1 <= int(ci) <= len(self.cells):
            raise ValueError(f"cell {ci} outside 1..{len(self.cells)}")
        return self.cells[int(ci) - 1]

    def rep_sizes(self, tag: str) -> tuple:
        return dict(self.rep_structures)[tag]


SPEC = ABSpec()


def small(spec: ABSpec = SPEC, **kw) -> ABSpec:
    """Test scale only (never used by the CLI): smaller B / reps; every rule number stays."""
    return dataclasses.replace(spec, **kw)
```

### Task 2: `ab_store` — the only writer

**Parallel group:** P1 (with T3, T4, T5). **Depends on:** T1.

**Files:** Create `flymon/brain/ab_store.py`, `tests/brain/test_ab_store.py`.

**Spec:** AB.2 6651 (writes only `results/ab/` + `results/summary/ab_learning.json`, atomic, cache = `r_store.RCache` with the W measurement key, archive `~/flymon-archive/ab/` never overwritten); AB.7 8 6819 (temporary name → rename; the archive copy skipped on the same sha, else `STOP_MACHINE`).

**Interfaces — produces:**
- `ALLOWED_DIR`, `SUMMARY`, `refuse`, `to_json`, `guard`;
- `write_bytes(path, data, params_list, exclusive=False)`, `write_json(path, obj, params_list, exclusive=False)`;
- `read_summary(path)`, `append_ledger(path, entry, params_list, which)`, `write_summary_block(path, block, obj, params_list, ledger, records_ledger, candidates, extra)`;
- `archive(files, dest, s)`;
- `ABCache(root, code, smoke_seeds)` (write-once `put`), `ReadCache(root, code)`;
- `invalid_slot`, `move_aside`, `write_invalid`, `remove_summary`.

This is `aa_store` with AB's paths. The code below **is** that copy (validated: 22 passed at plan time).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** `tests/brain/test_ab_store.py`:

```python
"""ab_store: AB writes only under results/ab/ and results/summary/ab_learning.json (symlink escapes refused), atomic;
two ledgers and the candidates block; ABCache never overwrites a raw entry; ReadCache never writes; archives are never
overwritten."""
import dataclasses
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from flymon.brain import ab_store as S
from flymon.brain.ab_spec import SPEC
from flymon.brain.config import Params

P = [Params()]


@pytest.fixture
def cwd(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    return tmp_path


@pytest.mark.parametrize("bad", ["results/aa/x.json", "results/aa/cache/w_learn/x.json", "results/y/x.json",
                                 "results/z/x.json", "results/summary/aa_learning.json", "results/summary/y_learning.json",
                                 "results/summary/z_learning.json", "results/abx/x.json", "elsewhere.json"])
def test_guard_refuses_outside(cwd, bad):
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as e:
        S.write_json(bad, {}, P)
    assert e.value.code == 2 and not Path(bad).exists()


def test_symlink_escape_refused(cwd, tmp_path):
    other = tmp_path / "other"
    other.mkdir()
    (cwd / "results").mkdir()
    (cwd / "results/ab").symlink_to(other)
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as e:
        S.write_json("results/ab/x.json", {}, P)
    assert e.value.code == 2 and list(other.iterdir()) == []


def test_write_is_atomic_on_failure(cwd, monkeypatch):
    S.write_json("results/ab/a.json", {"x": 1}, P)
    before = Path("results/ab/a.json").read_bytes()

    def boom(*a, **k):
        raise OSError("simulated failure mid-write")
    monkeypatch.setattr(S.os, "replace", boom)
    with pytest.raises(OSError):
        S.write_json("results/ab/a.json", {"x": 2}, P)
    assert Path("results/ab/a.json").read_bytes() == before                    # old content intact
    assert sorted(p.name for p in Path("results/ab").iterdir()) == ["a.json"]   # no temporary file left
    with pytest.raises(OSError):
        S.write_json("results/ab/b.json", {"x": 3}, P)
    assert not Path("results/ab/b.json").exists()                              # no partial target


def test_exclusive_write_refuses_existing(cwd):
    S.write_json("results/ab/e.json", {"x": 1}, P, exclusive=True)
    before = Path("results/ab/e.json").read_bytes()
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as e:
        S.write_json("results/ab/e.json", {"x": 2}, P, exclusive=True)
    assert e.value.code == 2 and Path("results/ab/e.json").read_bytes() == before
    assert sorted(p.name for p in Path("results/ab").iterdir()) == ["e.json"]


def test_ledgers_append_and_candidates_replace(cwd):
    S.write_summary_block(S.SUMMARY, "a", 1, P, dict(stage="a"), candidates=[dict(key="k1")])
    S.write_summary_block(S.SUMMARY, "b", 2, P, dict(stage="b"), candidates=[dict(key="k2")])
    d = S.read_summary()
    assert [e["stage"] for e in d["budget"]["ledger"]] == ["a", "b"] and d["candidates"] == [dict(key="k2")]
    S.write_summary_block(S.SUMMARY, "c", 3, P, dict(stage="c"))                # candidates=None keeps it
    d = S.read_summary()
    assert [e["stage"] for e in d["budget"]["ledger"]] == ["a", "b", "c"] and d["candidates"] == [dict(key="k2")]
    assert (d["a"], d["b"], d["c"]) == (1, 2, 3)


def test_ledgers_and_candidates(cwd):
    S.write_summary_block(S.SUMMARY, "stage0", dict(outcome="PASS"), P, dict(stage="stage0", wall_s=1.0),
                          candidates=[dict(key="k", c=0, axis="b", state="untouched")])
    S.append_ledger(S.SUMMARY, dict(stage="reuse", env_mismatch=["numpy"], wall_s=0.0), P)
    S.write_summary_block(S.SUMMARY, "records", dict(outcome="PASS"), P, None, dict(stage="records", wall_s=2.0))
    d = S.read_summary()
    assert [e["stage"] for e in d["budget"]["ledger"]] == ["stage0", "reuse"]
    assert d["budget"]["records_ledger"][0]["wall_s"] == 2.0 and d["candidates"][0]["state"] == "untouched"


def test_abcache_put_once_then_refuses(cwd):
    c = S.ABCache(SPEC.cache_dir, {"key": "w"}, smoke_seeds=())
    ins = dict(probe_seeds=[88_100_000], pair="p", brain="R", block="screen")
    c.put("w_learn", ins, {"v": 1}, P)
    assert c.get("w_learn", ins) == {"v": 1}
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as e:
        c.put("w_learn", ins, {"v": 2}, P)                       # raw cache immutable
    assert e.value.code == 2 and c.get("w_learn", ins) == {"v": 1}


def test_abcache_concurrent_put_refused_not_overwritten(cwd, monkeypatch):
    """Another writer lands the entry between put's exists-check and its publish: the put refuses, the other entry
    stays."""
    c = S.ABCache(SPEC.cache_dir, {"key": "w"}, smoke_seeds=())
    ins = dict(probe_seeds=[88_100_000], pair="p", brain="R", block="screen")
    target = c._path("w_learn", ins)
    real_link = os.link

    def racing_link(src, dst):
        Path(dst).write_text(json.dumps({"key": c.key("w_learn", ins), "kind": "w_learn", "result": {"v": "other"}}))
        return real_link(src, dst)
    monkeypatch.setattr(S.os, "link", racing_link)
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as e:
        c.put("w_learn", ins, {"v": "mine"}, P)
    assert e.value.code == 2 and c.get("w_learn", ins) == {"v": "other"}
    assert [p.name for p in target.parent.iterdir()] == [target.name]          # temporary file removed


def test_abcache_smoke_scope(cwd):
    smoke = frozenset([88_040_000])
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        S.ABCache(SPEC.cache_dir, {"key": "w"}, smoke).put("w_learn", dict(probe_seeds=[88_040_000]), {}, P)
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        S.ABCache(SPEC.smoke_cache_dir, {"key": "w"}, smoke).put("w_learn", dict(probe_seeds=[88_100_000]), {}, P)


def test_readcache_never_writes(cwd):
    r = S.ReadCache("results/aa/cache", {"key": "w"})
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        r.put("w_learn", dict(probe_seeds=[62_000_000]), {}, P)
    assert not Path("results/aa").exists()


def test_archive_never_overwrites(cwd, tmp_path):
    s = dataclasses.replace(SPEC, archive_root=str(tmp_path / "arch"))
    S.write_json("results/ab/a.json", {"x": 1}, P)
    m = S.archive(["results/ab/a.json"], "screen", s)
    assert len(m) == 1 and S.archive(["results/ab/a.json"], "screen", s) == m      # idempotent repeat
    S.write_json("results/ab/a.json", {"x": 2}, P)
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        S.archive(["results/ab/a.json"], "screen", s)


def test_archive_different_file_set_refused(cwd, tmp_path):
    s = dataclasses.replace(SPEC, archive_root=str(tmp_path / "arch"))
    S.write_json("results/ab/a.json", {"x": 1}, P)
    S.write_json("results/ab/b.json", {"x": 2}, P)
    S.archive(["results/ab/a.json"], "screen", s)
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as e:
        S.archive(["results/ab/a.json", "results/ab/b.json"], "screen", s)
    assert e.value.code == 2 and not (tmp_path / "arch/screen/aa/b.json").exists()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("exists", [False, True])
def test_archive_refuses_escape_from_root(cwd, tmp_path, exists):
    s = dataclasses.replace(SPEC, archive_root=str(tmp_path / "arch"))
    S.write_json("results/ab/a.json", {"x": 1}, P)
    (tmp_path / "arch").mkdir()
    if exists:                                     # an existing destination outside the root holding the same set
        (tmp_path / "x/ab").mkdir(parents=True)
        (tmp_path / "x/ab/a.json").write_bytes(Path("results/ab/a.json").read_bytes())
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as e:
        S.archive(["results/ab/a.json"], "../x", s)
    assert e.value.code == 2
    assert (tmp_path / "x").exists() == exists and list((tmp_path / "arch").iterdir()) == []
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write** `flymon/brain/ab_store.py`:

```python
"""AB's only writer (AB.2 6651; Z.9.2 P1-5 code boundary — a copy of aa_store with AB's paths; aa_store / y_store /
z_store / w_store are never called to write): results/ab/ and results/summary/ab_learning.json only (realpath-checked, so a symlink escape refuses),
atomic writes (temporary file + rename, or + exclusive link for a
write-once entry), anything else SystemExit 2. The engine-output guards of every writer in this
repository run first. Two ledgers live in the summary's `budget` block: `ledger` (core, AB cap 24 h) and
`records_ledger` (records, 4 h; AB.7 예산); an env mismatch is appended to `ledger` as an entry with
`env_mismatch`. The `candidates` block (AB.3 6691) is replaced whenever a block is written with `candidates=`.
- ABCache: r_store.RCache under results/ab/ with the W measurement key; a raw entry is written once and never
  rewritten (AA.7 3 "원자료 캐시는 불변", AB.7 8).
- ReadCache: a read-only RCache (the AA differential test reads results/aa/cache by cache hits only; AB.7 0).
- archive(files, dest, s): one copy of a fixed file set under <s.archive_root>/<dest>/<parent>/<name>
  (r_store.archive_copy, sha-checked; "사본은 블록마다 · STOP 문장 전에", AB.7 머리). Never overwritten: a repeat is a
  no-op when the archive holds exactly that set byte-identically, anything else refuses (SystemExit 2).
- restart_preseal (plan Reading 15; before the first measurement only): move_aside renames results/ab and the archive
  root to <path>.invalid-<n> (nothing deleted), write_invalid writes the note / summary copy once into
  results/ab.invalid-<n>/, remove_summary removes an untracked summary — the only writes outside results/ab/."""
from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
import json
import os
import sys
import uuid
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from .h3_store import canonical, canonical_pretty, sha256_file
from .pool_bench import refuse_modified_engine_output, refuse_old_engine_output
from .r_store import RCache, archive_copy

ALLOWED_DIR = "results/ab/"
SUMMARY = "results/summary/ab_learning.json"


def refuse(msg: str, code: int = 2):
    print(f"refusing: {msg}", file=sys.stderr)
    raise SystemExit(code)


def _plain(o):
    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(o) and not isinstance(o, type):
        return _plain(dataclasses.asdict(o))
    if isinstance(o, dict):
        return {str(k): _plain(v) for k, v in o.items()}
    if isinstance(o, (list, tuple)):
        return [_plain(v) for v in o]
    if isinstance(o, np.ndarray):
        return _plain(o.tolist())
    if isinstance(o, np.generic):
        return o.item()
    return o


def to_json(obj):
    return json.loads(json.dumps(_plain(obj), sort_keys=True))


def guard(path, params_list) -> None:
    for p in params_list:
        refuse_old_engine_output(str(path), p.kc_kc_scale)
        refuse_modified_engine_output(str(path), p)
    rel = os.path.relpath(os.path.realpath(str(path)), os.path.realpath(os.getcwd())).replace(os.sep, "/")
    if not (rel.startswith(ALLOWED_DIR) or rel == SUMMARY):
        refuse(f"AB writes only under {ALLOWED_DIR} and {SUMMARY}, not {path}")


def write_bytes(path, data: bytes, params_list, exclusive: bool = False) -> Path:
    """Temporary file, then os.replace (atomic). exclusive=True publishes with os.link instead, which fails when the
    target exists, so a concurrent or earlier entry is refused (SystemExit 2) and never replaced; still atomic."""
    guard(path, params_list)
    p = Path(path)
    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    tmp = p.parent / f".{p.name}.{uuid.uuid4().hex}.tmp"
    try:
        tmp.write_bytes(data)
        if exclusive:
            try:
                os.link(tmp, p)
            except FileExistsError:
                refuse(f"{p} exists; this entry is written once and never rewritten")
        else:
            os.replace(tmp, p)
    finally:
        if tmp.exists():
            tmp.unlink()
    return p


def write_json(path, obj, params_list, exclusive: bool = False) -> Path:
    return write_bytes(path, (canonical_pretty(to_json(obj)) + "\n").encode(), params_list, exclusive)


def read_summary(path=SUMMARY) -> dict:
    p = Path(path)
    return json.loads(p.read_text()) if p.exists() else {}


def append_ledger(path, entry: dict, params_list, which: str = "ledger") -> Path:
    doc = read_summary(path)
    b = dict(doc.get("budget") or {})
    b[which] = list(b.get(which, [])) + [entry]
    doc["budget"] = b
    return write_json(path, doc, params_list)


def write_summary_block(path, block: str, obj, params_list, ledger: dict | None = None,
                        records_ledger: dict | None = None, candidates: list | None = None,
                        extra: dict | None = None) -> Path:
    """`extra`: further top-level keys written in the same atomic write (the defect procedure's `invalid` list)."""
    doc = read_summary(path)
    doc[block] = obj
    for k, v in (extra or {}).items():
        doc[k] = v
    if candidates is not None:
        doc["candidates"] = candidates
    b = dict(doc.get("budget") or {})
    if ledger is not None:
        b["ledger"] = list(b.get("ledger", [])) + [ledger]
    if records_ledger is not None:
        b["records_ledger"] = list(b.get("records_ledger", [])) + [records_ledger]
    if b:
        doc["budget"] = b
    return write_json(path, doc, params_list)


def archive(files: list, dest: str, s) -> list:
    """Copy `files` to <s.archive_root>/<dest>/; never overwrites (module docstring)."""
    missing = [f for f in files if not Path(f).exists()]
    if missing:
        refuse(f"archive {dest}: source(s) missing: {missing[:3]}")
    if not files:
        return []
    root = Path(os.path.expanduser(s.archive_root))
    d = root / dest
    root_r, d_r = root.resolve(), d.resolve()
    if d_r == root_r or root_r not in d_r.parents:
        refuse(f"the archive goes only under {root_r}, not {d_r}")
    if not d.exists():
        return archive_copy(list(files), d, root)
    want = {d / Path(f).parent.name / Path(f).name: f for f in files}
    have = {p for p in d.rglob("*") if p.is_file()}
    if have != set(want):
        refuse(f"archive {d} holds another file set; an archive is never overwritten")
    out = []
    for dst, f in want.items():
        sha = sha256_file(f)
        if sha256_file(dst) != sha:
            refuse(f"archive {d} differs at {f}; an archive is never overwritten")
        out.append(dict(src=str(f), dst=str(dst), sha256=sha))
    return out


class ABCache(RCache):
    """RCache under results/ab/ (r_store's smoke-scope rule; put through this writer). A raw entry is written once — a
    second put of the same key refuses (SystemExit 2) instead of replacing it (AB.7 8: atomic, never overwritten)."""

    def __init__(self, root, code: dict, smoke_seeds=()):
        super().__init__(root, code, smoke_seeds)

    def put(self, kind, inputs, result, params_list) -> None:
        p = self._path(kind, inputs)
        if p.exists():
            refuse(f"{p}: the AB raw cache is immutable (AB.7 8); an entry is never rewritten")
        # exclusive publish: a concurrent put that lands between the check and the write is refused, not replaced
        write_json(p, {"key": self.key(kind, inputs), "kind": kind, "inputs": json.loads(canonical(inputs)),
                       "result": result}, params_list, exclusive=True)


class ReadCache(RCache):
    """A read-only RCache (the AA differential test reads results/aa/cache by W measurement key; AB.7 0)."""

    def __init__(self, root, code: dict):
        super().__init__(root, code, ())

    def put(self, kind, inputs, result, params_list) -> None:
        refuse(f"{self.root} is read-only for AB (cache hits only, AB.7 0)")


# ---- restart_preseal (plan Reading 15, before the first measurement) -------------------------------------------------
def _rel(p) -> str:
    return os.path.relpath(os.path.realpath(str(p)), os.path.realpath(os.getcwd())).replace(os.sep, "/")


def _raw_root(raw_dir) -> Path:
    if _rel(raw_dir) != ALLOWED_DIR.rstrip("/"):
        refuse(f"restart_preseal moves only {ALLOWED_DIR.rstrip('/')}, not {raw_dir}")
    return Path(raw_dir)


def _arch_root(archive_root) -> Path:
    p = Path(os.path.expanduser(archive_root))
    home = Path(os.path.expanduser("~")).resolve()
    if not p.name or p.resolve() in (Path("/"), home) or p.is_symlink():
        refuse(f"the pre-seal restart does not move the archive root {p}")
    return p


def invalid_slot(raw_dir, archive_root) -> int:
    """The smallest n ≥ 1 with neither <raw_dir>.invalid-<n> nor <archive_root>.invalid-<n> present."""
    raw, arch = _raw_root(raw_dir), _arch_root(archive_root)
    n = 1
    while raw.with_name(f"{raw.name}.invalid-{n}").exists() or arch.with_name(f"{arch.name}.invalid-{n}").exists():
        n += 1
    return n


def move_aside(path, n: int, archive: bool = False):
    """results/ab (or, archive=True, the AB archive root) → <path>.invalid-<n> (a rename, nothing deleted); None when
    the source is absent. Any other source, a symlink, or an existing destination refuses (SystemExit 2)."""
    import shutil
    p = _arch_root(path) if archive else _raw_root(path)
    if p.is_symlink():
        refuse(f"{p} is a symlink; the pre-seal restart moves only a real directory")
    if not p.exists():
        return None
    dst = p.with_name(f"{p.name}.invalid-{int(n)}")
    if dst.exists():
        refuse(f"{dst} exists; an invalid copy is never overwritten")
    shutil.move(str(p), str(dst))
    return dict(src=str(p), dst=str(dst))


def write_invalid(raw_dir, n: int, name: str, data: bytes) -> Path:
    """A write-once file directly in <raw_dir>.invalid-<n>/ (the restart note, the summary copy)."""
    raw = _raw_root(raw_dir)
    d = raw.with_name(f"{raw.name}.invalid-{int(n)}")
    if "/" in name or name.startswith("."):
        refuse(f"bad file name {name!r}")
    d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    p = d / name
    tmp = d / f".{name}.{uuid.uuid4().hex}.tmp"
    try:
        tmp.write_bytes(data)
        try:
            os.link(tmp, p)
        except FileExistsError:
            refuse(f"{p} exists; this entry is written once and never rewritten")
    finally:
        if tmp.exists():
            tmp.unlink()
    return p


def remove_summary(path, params_list) -> bool:
    """Removes results/summary/ab_learning.json (an untracked summary at a restart); True if it existed."""
    guard(path, params_list)
    if _rel(path) != SUMMARY:
        refuse(f"only {SUMMARY} is removed, not {path}")
    p = Path(path)
    if not p.exists():
        return False
    p.unlink()
    return True
```

### Task 3: `ab_rules` — labels, AB.8 sentences, reasons, budget arithmetic

**Parallel group:** P1. **Depends on:** T1 (names only; no import of `ab_spec` at module level).

**Files:** Create `flymon/brain/ab_rules.py`, `tests/brain/test_ab_rules.py`.

**Spec:**
- AB.8 6843–6882: the labels, the table, every sentence verbatim with the 〈…〉 slots filled.
- AB.8 6845: every STOP sentence ends with the five state counts and "이 지렛대의 M2 판정 트랙은 닫힌다(AB.9 닫힘 규칙)".
- AB.2 6647: the reuse reasons. AB.7 3 6810: KC repro. AB.7 6 6813: oracle machine checks.
- AB.7 예산 6826: 24 h, × 2.0.
- AB.3 6691: the five states.

**Interfaces — produces:**
- `PASS FAIL UNDECIDED INVALID STOP_*`, `STOPS`, `VERDICTS`, `STATES`, `CLOSURE`, `RECORDS_REASON`, `GATE_KO`;
- `counts(cands)`, `tail(c)`;
- `reuse_stop`, `set_mismatch_stop`, `calibration_stop`, `kc_repro_stop`, `budget_stop`, `machine_stop`, `few_pairs_oracle`, `few_pairs_screen`, `coverage_stop`, `protocol_stop` — each returns `{outcome, reasons, sentence, candidates_count, stop_stage}`;
- `head`, `pass_sentence`, `fail_sentence`, `undecided_sentence`;
- `reuse_reasons(facts, s)`, `kc_repro_diffs(odours, v_kc, got)`, `oracle_machine_reasons(results, rows, seeds, n_rep)`, `reason_counts(rows, ys)`;
- `core_ok`, `spent_ok`, `budget_text`, `spent_text`;
- re-exported `env_diff`, `env_reasons` (from `aa_rules`).

**Notes for the implementer:**
- The FAIL / UNDECIDED "… 같은 앞부분 …" is `head(k, gx, gt)` = the PASS sentence up to "F 8 × K 8)에서" (plan reading 6 of the Plan readings list).
- The PASS sentence orders its numbers as AB.8 6869 does: 보상 수준 · 보상 연합 > +1 first, then 처벌 하락 · 처벌 연합 < −1. `D_lim` etc. are GATES-ordered, hence the index order 0, 2, 1, 3.

```python
"""AB.8 sentences (verbatim slots), the candidate tail and closure rule, reuse / KC / oracle reasons, the budget
arithmetic (24 h, × 2.0)."""
import dataclasses

from flymon.brain import ab_rules as R
from flymon.brain.ab_spec import SPEC
from flymon.brain.y_spec import SPEC as Y

C = dict(untouched=150, oracled=10, screened=20, training=0, trained=4)


def test_tail_and_every_stop_ends_with_counts_and_closure():
    t = R.tail(C)
    assert t == ("(후보 상태: `untouched` 150 · `oracled` 10 · `screened` 20 · `training` 0 · `trained` 4) "
                 "이 지렛대의 M2 판정 트랙은 닫힌다(AB.9 닫힘 규칙).")
    stops = [R.reuse_stop(["x"], C), R.set_mismatch_stop("0c", "n_combos: 1 · 2", C), R.set_mismatch_stop("4", "d", C),
             R.calibration_stop("g7", (7, 6, 4, 3, 2, 2, 1), "R", "sel", 19, 0.031, 120, 5000, None, C),
             R.calibration_stop("g7", (7, 6, 4, 3, 2, 2, 1), "D", "ver", 19, 0.026, 230, 10000, 0.0001, C),
             R.kc_repro_stop(list(SPEC.kc_repro), ["none · ELECTRIC|GROUND: 0.1 대 0.2"], C),
             R.budget_stop("0e 전", "0.30 h + 2.0 × 12.00 h = 24.30 h", "2세대 측정 없음", C),
             R.machine_stop("오라클", "단위 수 1 ≠ 2", C, "6"), R.few_pairs_oracle(150, 7, 4, C),
             R.few_pairs_screen(30, 7, 5, 6, dict(balance=10, a_floor=12, p_floor=1), C),
             R.coverage_stop(20, (5, 4, 3, 3, 2, 2, 1), (3,) * 6 + (2,), "R", "ver", 21, 0.026, C),
             R.protocol_stop("a|3|Surf|Earthquake", [0.7, 0.9], C)]
    for s in stops:
        assert s["sentence"].endswith(R.CLOSURE) and s["outcome"] in R.STOPS and s["candidates_count"] == C
    assert "선택: 격자 끝 α 0.0001에서도 칸 19의 CP 97.5% 상한 0.031 > 0.025(120/5 000)" in stops[3]["sentence"]
    assert "검증: 고른 α 0.0001에서 칸 19의 CP 상한 0.026 > 0.025(230/10 000)" in stops[4]["sentence"]
    assert stops[1]["sentence"].startswith("AB 생성원(AB.3)이 선언값 n_combos: 1 · 2과 다르다 — 측정 없음.")
    assert "KC 입력만 측정(냄새 값, L_V 판정 결과 아님)" in stops[2]["sentence"]


def _pass_fill():
    return dict(k=20, gx=6, gt=9, D_lim=[3.1, -2.9, 3.4, -2.5], Dfin_lim=[3.0, -2.8, 3.3, -2.4],
                R_lim=[1.2, -0.9, 1.5, -0.8], D_pt=[5.0, -4.0, 5.5, -3.9], aD=0.0001, aF=0.00025, aR=0.0001, k_fin=20,
                df=5, share=[0.8, 0.7, 0.8, 0.6], n10=[30, 12, 25, 10], ninf=[4, 1, 3, 0], n_flies=160, phi_R=0.0,
                phi_P=0.125)


def test_pass_fail_undecided_sentences():
    p = R.pass_sentence(_pass_fill())
    assert p.startswith("조합 지렛대 모델(C3, APL→MBON05 2간선 + MBON05→MBON09/MBON11/MBON01 11간선 제거, E-grid k2-norm "
                        "s 1.0, z_V)에서 실제 학습 규칙(F.2 R · N + G.5 RN)은 2세대 넓힌 풀 AB 세트의 Y 거름 통과 20쌍(X "
                        "라벨 묶음 6개 · 상대 타입 집합 묶음 9개, F 8 × K 8)에서 네 관문 모두 효과 크기 기준을 넘었다")
    assert "보상 수준 3.1 · 보상 연합 3.4 > +1, 처벌 하락 -2.9 · 처벌 연합 -2.5 < −1" in p
    assert "POOL 안 확인이 아니며 M3를 자동으로 열지 않는다(T.9.5, AB.9)." in p and "df 5" in p
    f = R.fail_sentence(dict(k=20, gx=6, gt=9, gate="reward_assoc", sign=1.0, vals=[0.4, 0.5], aF=0.00025,
                             past_zero=False))
    assert "관문 보상 연합에서 두 방식 모두의 상한 0.4 · 0.5 < +1" in f and "0은 기대 방향으로 넘었다" in f
    u = R.undecided_sentence(dict(k=20, gx=6, gt=9, causes=["관문 punish_assoc의 R 구간이 ±0.25 z를 걸침"]))
    assert "PASS도 FAIL도 아니다 — 관문 punish_assoc의 R 구간이 ±0.25 z를 걸침." in u and R.CLOSURE in u


def _facts():
    return dict(v_anc={c: True for _, c in SPEC.v_commits}, aa_anc={c: True for _, c in SPEC.aa_blocks},
                kc_sha=SPEC.kc_input_sha256, w_digest=dict(digest_keys=SPEC.w_digest_keys, n_b=167, n_a=82,
                                                         last_turn=1967),
                aa_doc={b: dict(outcome="PASS") for b, _ in SPEC.aa_blocks}, lv=dict(reasons=[]), v_check=[],
                keys=dict(w_measure_key=Y.w_measure_key, u_measure_key=Y.u_measure_key),
                want_keys=dict(w_measure_key=Y.w_measure_key, u_measure_key=Y.u_measure_key),
                decl_sha={"flymon/brain/w_spec.py": "a"}, now_sha={"flymon/brain/w_spec.py": "a"})


def test_reuse_reasons():
    assert R.reuse_reasons(_facts(), SPEC) == []
    f = _facts()
    f["aa_doc"]["learn"] = dict(outcome="STOP_BUDGET")
    f["kc_sha"] = "x"
    f["now_sha"] = {"flymon/brain/w_spec.py": "b"}
    f["lv"] = dict(reasons=["lv_odour 냄새 107개 ≠ 108"])
    why = R.reuse_reasons(f, SPEC)
    assert len(why) == 4 and any("AA 블록 learn" in w for w in why) and any("lv_odour" in w for w in why)
    assert R.reuse_reasons(dict(_facts(), decl_sha={}), SPEC)[0].startswith("ea408a2 시점")


def test_kc_repro_and_budget():
    v = {"none": {"A|B": 0.05}, "lever": {"A|B": 0.04}}
    assert R.kc_repro_diffs(["A|B"], v, v) == []
    assert R.kc_repro_diffs(["A|B"], v, {"none": {"A|B": 0.05}, "lever": {"A|B": 0.0400001}}) == \
        ["lever · A|B: 0.0400001 대 0.04"]
    assert R.core_ok(4.0, 10.0, SPEC) and not R.core_ok(4.0, 10.001, SPEC)
    assert R.budget_text(0.3, 10.3, SPEC) == "0.30 h + 2.0 × 10.30 h = 20.90 h"
    assert R.reason_counts([dict(d=0.6, L_A=19.9999999999, L_P=50)], Y) == dict(balance=1, a_floor=0, p_floor=0)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- the AB.8 text itself (located by content)
import re  # noqa: E402
from pathlib import Path  # noqa: E402

SPEC_MD = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-14-flymon-design.md"


def _pattern(t: str) -> str:
    t = t.replace("**", "").replace("… 같은 앞부분 … ", "")
    out, depth, buf = "", 0, ""
    for ch in t:
        if ch == "〈":
            if depth == 0:
                out, buf = out + re.escape(buf) + ".*?", ""
            depth += 1
        elif ch == "〉":
            depth -= 1
        elif depth == 0:
            buf += ch
    return out + re.escape(buf)


def _quoted(prefix: str) -> str:
    lines = [ln for ln in SPEC_MD.read_text().splitlines() if ln.startswith(prefix)]
    assert len(lines) == 1, prefix
    return re.search(r'"(.*)"', lines[0]).group(1)


def test_every_sentence_matches_ab8_verbatim():
    strip = lambda s: s.split(" (후보 상태")[0]                       # noqa: E731
    cases = [("- **`STOP_REUSE`**(0b):", strip(R.reuse_stop(["x"], C)["sentence"])),
             ("- **`STOP_SET_MISMATCH`**(0c · 4):", strip(R.set_mismatch_stop("0c", "a: 1 · 2", C)["sentence"])),
             ("- **`STOP_CALIBRATION`**〈0e〉:",
              strip(R.calibration_stop("g7", (7, 6), "R", "sel", 19, 0.031, 120, 5000, None, C)["sentence"])),
             ("- **`STOP_KC_REPRO`**(3):", strip(R.kc_repro_stop(["A"], ["d"], C)["sentence"])),
             ("- **`STOP_BUDGET`**(0e 전", strip(R.budget_stop("0e 전", "a", "b", C)["sentence"])),
             ("- **`STOP_MACHINE`**(6 · 7 · 8):", strip(R.machine_stop("오라클", "x", C, "6")["sentence"])),
             ("- **`STOP_FEW_PAIRS`〈오라클〉**(6):", strip(R.few_pairs_oracle(150, 7, 4, C)["sentence"])),
             ("- **`STOP_FEW_PAIRS`〈거름〉**(7):",
              strip(R.few_pairs_screen(30, 7, 5, 6, dict(balance=1, a_floor=2, p_floor=0), C)["sentence"])),
             ("- **`STOP_COVERAGE`**(7b):", strip(R.coverage_stop(20, (5,), (3,), "R", "ver", 21, 0.03, C)["sentence"])),
             ("- **`STOP_PROTOCOL`**(9, F.7 행):", strip(R.protocol_stop("a|3", [0.7, 0.9], C)["sentence"])),
             ("- **`PASS` 문장(AB.10에 그대로)**:", R.pass_sentence(_pass_fill()))]
    for prefix, text in cases:
        assert re.fullmatch(_pattern(_quoted(prefix)), text.replace("**", ""), re.S), prefix
    for prefix, text in (("- **`FAIL` 문장**:", R.fail_sentence(dict(k=20, gx=6, gt=9, gate="reward_assoc", sign=1.0,
                                                                   vals=[0.4, 0.5], aF=0.00025, past_zero=True))),
                         ("- **`UNDECIDED` 문장**:", R.undecided_sentence(dict(k=20, gx=6, gt=9, causes=["c"])))):
        body = text.replace("**", "").split("F 8 × K 8)에서 ", 1)[1]
        assert re.fullmatch(_pattern(_quoted(prefix)), body, re.S), prefix
```
