```python


# ================================================================ calibration (AB.5)
def _skew(zv, sd):
    return sd * (np.exp(zv) - math.exp(1 / 2)) / math.sqrt(math.e * (math.e - 1))


def effects(cell: tuple, gx, gt, r, s=AB) -> np.ndarray:
    """u_p of one replicate (structure-aware draws; draw order fixed by the cell kind)."""
    kind, a = cell
    P, Gx, Gt = len(gx), int(np.max(gx)) + 1, int(np.max(gt)) + 1
    if kind == "X":
        return r.normal(0.0, a, Gx)[gx]
    if kind == "T":
        return r.normal(0.0, a, Gt)[gt]
    if kind == "pair":
        return r.normal(0.0, a, P)
    if kind == "XT":
        return r.normal(0.0, a, Gx)[gx] + r.normal(0.0, a, Gt)[gt]
    if kind == "Xpair":
        return r.normal(0.0, a, Gx)[gx] + r.normal(0.0, a, P)
    if kind == "skX":
        return a * _skew(r.standard_normal(Gx), s.skew_sd)[gx]
    if kind == "skT":
        return a * _skew(r.standard_normal(Gt), s.skew_sd)[gt]
    return np.zeros(P)


def marginal_effects(cell: tuple, n: int, r, s=AB) -> np.ndarray:
    """u for n independent pairs from the cell's marginal (the truth's superpopulation, plan Reading 8)."""
    kind, a = cell
    if kind in ("X", "T", "pair"):
        return r.normal(0.0, a, n)
    if kind in ("XT", "Xpair"):
        return r.normal(0.0, a, n) + r.normal(0.0, a, n)
    if kind in ("skX", "skT"):
        return a * _skew(r.standard_normal(n), s.skew_sd)
    return np.zeros(n)


def probes(cell: tuple, u, r, dlt: float, s=AB) -> np.ndarray:
    """[P, F, K] probe contrasts = δ + u_p + m_{p,f} + N(0, 1); cell (23)/(24): flies 0–1 constant ±0.05."""
    u = np.asarray(u, float)
    P = u.size
    fe = r.standard_normal((P, s.flies, 1)) if cell[0] == "fly" else 0.0
    x = dlt + u[:, None, None] + fe + r.standard_normal((P, s.flies, s.probes))
    if cell[0] == "floor":
        x[:, :s.floor_flies, :] = s.floor_const * cell[1]
    return x


def synth_stats(x, s=AB) -> dict:
    return stats_from_dprime(WV.dprime(x), x.mean(-1), s)


def truth(st, tag: str, ci: int, s=AB, n=None) -> dict:
    """AB.5 참값: the mean pair statistic over n simulated pairs (stream ("cal", tag, "truth", ci))."""
    check_structure(st)
    n = s.truth_pairs if n is None else int(n)
    r = AE.stream(s.cal_seed, "cal", tag, "truth", int(ci))
    cell, dl = s.cell(ci), delta(s)
    acc, cnt, m = dict(D=0.0, Dfin=0.0, R=0.0), dict(D=0, Dfin=0, R=0), 0
    while m < n:
        c = min(s.truth_chunk, n - m)
        x = probes(cell, marginal_effects(cell, c, r, s), r, dl, s)
        st3 = synth_stats(x, s)
        for name in STATS:
            th = AE._nanmean(st3[name], 1)                      # a pair without a finite fly leaves D_fin's mean
            acc[name] += float(np.nansum(th))
            cnt[name] += int(np.isfinite(th).sum())
        m += c
    return {k: acc[k] / cnt[k] for k in STATS}


def rep_misses(st, cell, r, tru: dict, s=AB, B=None) -> dict:
    """One replicate: {stat: {P: joint [Lp], P_TS, P_CG, (D) F: joint [Lf], F_TS, F_CG}} booleans."""
    gx, gt = _labels(st)
    groups = groups_of(gx)
    x = probes(cell, effects(cell, gx, gt, r, s), r, delta(s), s)
    st3 = synth_stats(x, s)
    out = {}
    for name in STATS:
        X = st3[name]
        lv = list(s.p_grid) + (list(s.f_grid) if name == "D" else [])
        reps = ts_reps(X[..., None], groups, r, s, B)
        lo, hi = ts_limits(reps, lv, s)
        clo, chi = cg_limits(AE._nanmean(X, 1), gx, gt, lv)
        t = tru[name]
        up_ts, up_cg = lo[:, 0] > t, clo > t
        dn_ts, dn_cg = hi[:, 0] < t, chi < t
        n_p = len(s.p_grid)
        row = dict(P=(up_ts & up_cg)[:n_p], P_TS=up_ts[:n_p], P_CG=up_cg[:n_p])
        if name == "D":
            row.update(F=(dn_ts & dn_cg)[n_p:], F_TS=dn_ts[n_p:], F_CG=dn_cg[n_p:])
        out[name] = row
    return out


def _zero_counts(s) -> dict:
    out = {}
    for name in STATS:
        out[name] = {k: [0] * len(s.p_grid) for k in ("P", "P_TS", "P_CG")}
        if name == "D":
            out[name].update({k: [0] * len(s.f_grid) for k in ("F", "F_TS", "F_CG")})
    return out


def _gen_from(state: dict | None, s, tag, phase, ci):
    r = AE.stream(s.cal_seed, "cal", tag, phase, int(ci))
    if state is not None:
        r.bit_generator.state = state
    return r


def cal_run(st, tag: str, phase: str, ci: int, tru: dict, s=AB, n=None, resume=None, on_chunk=None,
            B=None, only=None) -> dict:
    """Reps of one (structure tag, phase, cell) on stream ("cal", tag, phase, ci), in chunks of cal_chunk. resume =
    a previous payload {done, counts, state}; on_chunk(payload) is called after every chunk (the runner's atomic
    checkpoint). only = {stat: {side: α}} (verification): only those levels are counted, so the verification stream
    never informs the selection (AB.5 (나)). Returns the final payload. Bit-identical whether run at once or
    resumed."""
    check_structure(st)
    if phase not in ("sel", "ver"):
        raise ValueError(phase)
    n = (s.n_sel if phase == "sel" else s.n_ver) if n is None else int(n)
    pay = dict(done=0, n=n, counts=_zero_counts(s), state=None) if resume is None else copy.deepcopy(resume)
    r = _gen_from(pay["state"], s, tag, phase, ci)
    cell = s.cell(ci)
    while pay["done"] < n:
        m = min(s.cal_chunk, n - pay["done"])
        for _ in range(m):
            got = rep_misses(st, cell, r, tru, s, B)
            for name, row in got.items():
                for k, v in row.items():
                    keep = None
                    if only is not None:
                        a = (only.get(name) or {}).get(k[0])
                        keep = None if a is None else _grid(k[0], s).index(a)
                        if keep is None:
                            continue
                    c = pay["counts"][name][k]
                    for i, b in enumerate(np.asarray(v, bool).tolist()):
                        if keep is None or i == keep:
                            c[i] += int(b)
        pay = dict(pay, done=pay["done"] + m, state=r.bit_generator.state)
        if on_chunk is not None:
            on_chunk(copy.deepcopy(pay))
    return pay


def cp_upper(x: int, n: int, s=AB) -> float:
    """Clopper–Pearson one-sided upper bound at cp_level (scipy.stats.beta); x = n → 1."""
    return 1.0 if x >= n else float(stats.beta.ppf(s.cp_level, x + 1, n - x))


def _grid(side: str, s) -> tuple:
    return s.p_grid if side == "P" else s.f_grid


def _target(side: str, s) -> float:
    return s.p_target if side == "P" else s.f_target


def select(counts: dict, n: int, s=AB) -> dict:
    """(가) per stat and side: the largest α (grids run large → small) whose CP upper bound ≤ target in every cell.
    counts = {ci: payload counts}. Returns {stat: {P: α | None, (D) F: α | None}} + the per-cell bounds."""
    out = {}
    for name in STATS:
        out[name] = {}
        for side in (("P", "F") if name == "D" else ("P",)):
            pick = None
            for i, a in enumerate(_grid(side, s)):
                if all(_r(cp_upper(c[name][side][i], n, s) - _target(side, s), s) <= 0 for c in counts.values()):
                    pick = a
                    break
            out[name][side] = pick
    return out


def verify(counts: dict, chosen: dict, n: int, s=AB) -> dict:
    """{stat: {side: {alpha, ok, worst: {cell, x, cp}}}} at the chosen α only (None = not reached)."""
    out = {}
    for name, sides in chosen.items():
        out[name] = {}
        for side, a in sides.items():
            if a is None:
                out[name][side] = dict(alpha=None, ok=False, worst=None)
                continue
            i = _grid(side, s).index(a)
            cps = {ci: cp_upper(c[name][side][i], n, s) for ci, c in counts.items()}
            w = max(cps, key=lambda k: cps[k])
            out[name][side] = dict(alpha=a, ok=all(_r(v - _target(side, s), s) <= 0 for v in cps.values()),
                                   worst=dict(cell=int(w), x=int(counts[w][name][side][i]), cp=float(cps[w])))
    return out


def _serial_cell(kind, args):
    """The default cell executor (no checkpoint): kind "truth" → truth(*args), "sel"/"ver" → cal_run(*args)."""
    if kind == "truth":
        return truth(*args)
    return cal_run(*args)


def calibrate_many(items: list, s=AB, cell=None) -> list:
    """AB.5 선택과 검증 on several structures at once: items = [(structure, tag)] (structures only — anything else
    TypeError). cell(kind, args) runs one job (the runner passes a checkpointing, pooled executor with .many); each
    phase runs across every item together: 24 truths per item, then 24 selection cells, then 24 verification cells
    (only at the chosen α). Returns one summary per item, in order."""
    if not isinstance(items, list) or not items:
        raise TypeError("calibrate_many takes a non-empty list of (structure, tag)")
    for st, tag in items:
        check_structure(st)
        if not isinstance(tag, str):
            raise TypeError("the structure tag is a string")
    run = cell or _serial_cell
    cis = list(range(1, len(s.cells) + 1))
    idx = [(i, ci) for i in range(len(items)) for ci in cis]
    got = run_many(run, [("truth", (items[i][0], items[i][1], ci, s)) for i, ci in idx])
    tr = [{} for _ in items]
    for (i, ci), v in zip(idx, got):
        tr[i][ci] = v
    got = run_many(run, [("sel", (items[i][0], items[i][1], "sel", ci, tr[i][ci], s)) for i, ci in idx])
    sel = [{} for _ in items]
    for (i, ci), v in zip(idx, got):
        sel[i][ci] = v["counts"]
    chosen = [select(sel[i], s.n_sel, s) for i in range(len(items))]
    only = [{n: {sd: a for sd, a in v.items() if a is not None} for n, v in ch.items()} for ch in chosen]
    need = [i for i in range(len(items)) if any(a is not None for sd in chosen[i].values() for a in sd.values())]
    vidx = [(i, ci) for i in need for ci in cis]
    got = run_many(run, [("ver", (items[i][0], items[i][1], "ver", ci, tr[i][ci], s, None, None, None, None, only[i]))
                         for i, ci in vidx])
    ver = [{} for _ in items]
    for (i, ci), v in zip(vidx, got):
        ver[i][ci] = v["counts"]
    out = []
    for i, (st, tag) in enumerate(items):
        checked = verify(ver[i], chosen[i], s.n_ver, s) if i in need else {
            n: {sd: dict(alpha=None, ok=False, worst=None) for sd in v} for n, v in chosen[i].items()}
        alpha = {n: {sd: (v["alpha"] if v["ok"] else None) for sd, v in sides.items()} for n, sides in checked.items()}
        sel_cp = {n: {sd: {ci: [cp_upper(x, s.n_sel, s) for x in sel[i][ci][n][sd]] for ci in cis}
                      for sd in (("P", "F") if n == "D" else ("P",))} for n in STATS}
        out.append(dict(tag=tag, structure=[list(p) for p in st], k=len(st), groups=list(n_groups(st)), truth=tr[i],
                        sel_counts=sel[i], ver_counts=ver[i], chosen=chosen[i], verified=checked, alpha=alpha,
                        pass_ok=all(alpha[n]["P"] is not None for n in STATS), fail_ok=alpha["D"]["F"] is not None,
                        sel_cp=sel_cp, n_sel=s.n_sel, n_ver=s.n_ver))
    return out


def calibrate(st, tag: str, s=AB, cell=None) -> dict:
    """AB.5 on one structure (tuple of (X group, type-set group) int pairs only; anything else TypeError)."""
    check_structure(st)
    return calibrate_many([(st, tag)], s, cell)[0]


def run_many(run, jobs: list) -> list:
    """Jobs through the executor: a callable with .many(jobs) runs them together (the runner's pool), else one by
    one."""
    many = getattr(run, "many", None)
    return many(jobs) if many is not None else [run(k, a) for k, a in jobs]


# ================================================================ bench and the synthetic validation (AB.7 0, AB.5)
def bench(s=AB) -> dict:
    """AB.7 0 비용 측정: g 7 cells (17) · (23), bench_reps reps each, B = boot_b, seconds per rep (stream ("bench",
    cell) under cal_seed; never a calibration count)."""
    st = rep_structure(s.rep_sizes("g7"), s)
    out = {}
    for ci in s.bench_cells:
        r = AE.stream(s.cal_seed, "bench", int(ci))
        tru = dict(D=1.0, Dfin=1.0, R=1.0)
        t = time.perf_counter()
        for _ in range(s.bench_reps):
            rep_misses(st, s.cell(ci), r, tru, s)
        out[str(ci)] = (time.perf_counter() - t) / s.bench_reps
    return dict(per_rep_s=out, max_s=max(out.values()), structure="g7", B=s.boot_b, reps=s.bench_reps)


def synth_configs(s=AB) -> list:
    """(name, per-gate effect in the expected direction) — AB.5 합성 검증 (1)–(3); (1) puts reward_level at the bar
    (by the mirror, any gate gives the same distribution — plan Reading 10)."""
    d0 = delta(s)
    cfg = [("bar_one", (d0,) + (s.synth_other,) * 3), ("bar_all", (d0,) * 4)]
    cfg += [(f"eff{e}", (float(e),) * 4) for e in s.synth_effects]
    return cfg


def synth_rep(st, cell, eff: tuple, alpha: dict, r, s=AB, B=None) -> str:
    """One synthetic replicate of the whole verdict: four independent gates (sign × (effect + u + noise)), then
    set_limits on stream r and verdict with no machine / mechanism problems; returns the label."""
    gx, gt = _labels(st)
    keys = [f"a|{i}|X{int(gx[i])}|Y" for i in range(len(gx))]
    tsets = [f"T{int(gt[i])}" for i in range(len(gx))]
    per = {k: dict(D=np.empty((s.flies, 4)), Dfin=np.empty((s.flies, 4)), R=np.empty((s.flies, 4))) for k in keys}
    for g in range(4):
        x = SIGNS[g] * probes(cell, effects(cell, gx, gt, r, s), r, float(eff[g]), s)
        st3 = synth_stats(x, s)
        for i, k in enumerate(keys):
            for name in STATS:
                per[k][name][:, g] = st3[name][i]
    lv = dict(D=[alpha["D"]] + ([alpha["F"]] if alpha.get("F") is not None else []), Dfin=[alpha["Dfin"]],
              R=[alpha["R"]])
    lim = set_limits(per, keys, tsets, lv, s, B, rngs={name: r for name in STATS})
    return verdict(lim, [], {}, alpha, s)["label"]
```

**Key code (rule-bearing):**

```python
def closure(files) -> list:
    """Every flymon file reachable from `files` through relative imports (levels 1 and 2), recursively."""
    import ast
    seen, todo = set(), [str(f) for f in files]
    while todo:
        p = todo.pop()
        if p in seen or not (ROOT / p).exists():
            continue
        seen.add(p)
        base = Path(p).parent
        for n in ast.walk(ast.parse((ROOT / p).read_text())):
            if not isinstance(n, ast.ImportFrom) or n.level not in (1, 2):
                continue
            pkg = base if n.level == 1 else base.parent
            if n.module:
                mod = pkg.joinpath(*n.module.split("."))
                todo.append(f"{mod.as_posix()}.py")
                todo += [f"{mod.as_posix()}/{a.name}.py" for a in n.names]      # `from ..agent import e_pairs`
            else:
                todo += [f"{pkg.as_posix()}/{a.name}.py" for a in n.names]      # `from . import x`
    return sorted(seen)


def differential(ctx, s=AB) -> dict:
    """AB.7 0 6783: AA S1's first `diff_pairs` pairs in c order, assembled by ab_runner with AA's main-set seeds
    (aa_runner.aa_w_spec(): 62_000_000 / 64_000_000), read from results/aa/cache by W measurement key (cache hits
    only — a miss is a failure, never a measurement). Bit-equal: unit cache keys vs AA's learn manifest, count arrays
    vs AA's manifest raw, gate_stats / winsorized fly means / fly labels / the two raw-contrast means vs
    results/aa/records.json, the association means vs estimate.json; plus the TS path: ab_estimate.ts_reps +
    ts_limits at α 0.025 on all S1 pairs with AA's stream (87_000_000, "pool", "S1") = AA's pooled CIs."""
    aa = ctx["docs"]()["aa_learning"]
    s1 = [p for p in aa["screen"]["pairs"] if p["passed"]]
    first = sorted(s1, key=lambda p: int(p["c"]))[:s.diff_pairs]
    rows = {int(r["c"]): r for r in ctx["aa_rows"]()}
    learn = ctx["aa_detail"]("learn")["manifest"]
    man = {m["key"]: m for m in learn}
    wm = ctx["aa_reader"]()
    z = ctx["w_z"]()
    rec = ctx["aa_detail"]("records")["block"]["pairs"]
    est = ctx["aa_detail"]("estimate")["primary"]
    why, per = [], {}
    U = aa_runner.units([rows[int(p["c"])] for p in first], "main", s.probes, s.flies, V_SPEC.lever_edit,
                        ws=aa_runner.aa_w_spec())
    got = []
    for u in U:
        k = f"{u['pair']}|{u['fly']}|{u['brain']}"
        ins = wm.inputs(u, "learn")
        m = man.get(k)
        if m is None or wm.cache.key(KIND, ins) != m["cache_key"]:
            why.append(f"{k}: 캐시 키 ≠ AA learn 매니페스트")
        res = wm.cache.get(KIND, ins)
        if res is None:
            why.append(f"{k}: 캐시 적중 실패(새 측정 없음)")
            continue
        got.append(dict(unit=u, result=res))
    flies = list(range(s.flies))
    aa_got, bad = load_manifest(learn)
    why += [f"AA learn 원자료: {b}" for b in bad[:3]]
    aa_by = {}
    for g in aa_got:
        pair, fly, brain = g["key"].rsplit("|", 2)
        aa_by.setdefault(pair, []).append(dict(unit=dict(pair=pair, fly=int(fly), brain=brain), result=g["result"]))
    data = {k: w_records.pair_data(v, flies) for k, v in aa_by.items()}
    for k, rows_k in aa_runner.by_pair(got).items():
        mine = aa_runner.pair_counts(rows_k, flies)
        st = ab_estimate.pair_stats(mine, z, s)
        r_ = rec[k]
        ok = dict(
            counts=all(np.array_equal(mine[x], data[k][x]) for x in w_verdict.STAGES),
            means=all(float(st["D"][:, i].mean()) == r_["gates"][g]["mean"] for i, g in enumerate(w_verdict.GATES)),
            flies=all([aa_estimate._lab(v) for v in st["raw"][:, i]] == r_["gates"][g]["fly"]
                      for i, g in enumerate(w_verdict.GATES)),
            raw=all(float(st["R"][:, 2 + j].mean()) == r_["raw"][g]["mean"] for j, g in enumerate(aa_estimate.RAW)),
            estimate=all(float(st["D"][:, aa_estimate.GI[g]].mean()) == next(
                p for p in est["pairs"] if p["key"] == k)[g]["mean"] for g in aa_estimate.PRIMARY))
        per[k] = ok
        why += [f"{k}: {n} 불일치" for n, v in ok.items() if not v]
    keys = [p["key"] for p in s1]                          # AA's screen order = c order (AA estimate_set's keys)
    X = np.stack([ab_estimate.pair_stats(data[k], z, s)["D"] for k in keys])
    reps = ab_estimate.ts_reps(X, y_rules.merge_groups(keys), aa_estimate.stream(s.aa_boot_seed, "pool", "S1"), s)
    lo, hi = ab_estimate.ts_limits(reps, [0.025], s)          # AA's (2.5, 97.5) — a literal of the AA path; see note
    ts_ok = {g: [float(lo[0][aa_estimate.GI[g]]), float(hi[0][aa_estimate.GI[g]])] == est["pooled"][g]["ci"]
             for g in aa_estimate.PRIMARY}
    why += [f"TS {g}: AA pooled CI와 다름" for g, v in ts_ok.items() if not v]
    return dict(ok=not why, reasons=why, n_units=len(U), n_hits=len(got), pairs=per, ts=ts_ok)
```

The literal `0.025` violates the Task 1 guard. Use `s.p_grid[0]` instead (= 0.025 = AA's 2.5 / 100). The scratch run compared exactly this level.

**`stage_generate`** (0c, 6689, 6804):

```python
    def stage_generate(self) -> dict:
        doc = self._require("generate")
        s, t0 = self.s, time.perf_counter()
        lv = doc["reuse"]["lv_odour"]["odours"]                      # 0b's list only (6647)
        st = self.ctx["selftest"]()
        gen = self.ctx["generate"](lv)
        why = ab_pairs.declared_reasons(gen, s)
        if not st["ok"]:
            why.append(f"생성기 자가 시험: digest {st['digest_keys']} ≠ {s.w_digest_keys}")
        rec = {k: gen[k] for k in gen if k not in ("rows",)}
        p = ab_store.write_json(self._detail("generate"), dict(rec, selftest=st), self.plist)
        self._finish("generate", t0)
        if why:
            # AB.7 0c (해석 9): the code follows AB.3's wording (its tests pass), so a difference is a declared-value
            # defect → STOP_SET_MISMATCH〈0c〉. A code defect is found by the order-0 tests before this point; then
            # nothing is written (refuse, exit 2) and the fix goes through restart_preseal.
            if self.ctx.get("refuse_generate_mismatch"):
                refuse(f"stage generate: declared values differ: {why[:3]}")
            body = ab_rules.set_mismatch_stop("0c", "; ".join(why[:3]), self._counts(doc))
        else:
            body = dict(outcome=ab_rules.PASS, reasons=[])
        return self._write("generate", dict(body, **rec, selftest=st, detail_path=self._detail("generate"),
                                            detail_sha256=sha256_file(p),
                                            note="AB.3 · AB.7 0c: 측정 없음, 봉인 앞 — 선언값과 대조(선언 형식 계수, "
                                                 "plan Reading 2); 순서대로 센 계수는 skipped_inorder에 기록."))
```

The 0c refusal vs STOP split follows 해석 9. The operator decides between them: if the reviewer finds the generation code at fault, the fix is a pre-seal code fix through `restart_preseal`. Otherwise the outcome is `STOP_SET_MISMATCH`. The CLI never refuses by itself, and `refuse_generate_mismatch` is never set in production.

**`stage_seal_code`** (0d, 6805): `seal = seal_now(s)`, outcome PASS, plus the note "AB.7 0d: 2세대 첫 측정 전 봉인 — ab_estimate · ab_rules · ab_pairs + import 닫힘 + ab_spec 수치 + 흐름 시험 벡터". Its `stream_vectors` check: the block records the four vectors recomputed with `aa_estimate.stream` and refuses (exit 2) if they differ from `s.stream_vectors`.

**`restart_preseal(note_path)`** (Reading 15):
- **Refusals (exit 2):**
  - the working or HEAD summary holds `kc_input` or any later block, or a `defect_<n>` / `reseal_<n>` block;
  - `results/ab/cache/` has a file;
  - the note lacks non-empty `symptom` / `clause` / `cause`.
- **Moves:** n = `ab_store.invalid_slot`; `move_aside` `results/ab` and the archive root; the summary copy is written into `results/ab.invalid-<n>/`.
- **Summary rewrite:** `{**{k: v for k in doc if k.startswith("restart_preseal_")}, f"restart_preseal_{n}": dict(outcome="PASS", symptom, clause, cause, old_seal=(doc.get("seal_code") or {}).get("seal", {}).get("key"), invalidated={b: dict(doc[b], outcome_was=doc[b].get("outcome"), outcome="INVALID") for b in s.stages if b in doc}, at), budget}`. When HEAD does not track the summary and no block exists, it removes the summary instead.
- **Returns** `{outcome: PASS, sentence: "사전 측정 재시작 <n>: … 다음은 §2 시험 로그 → stage0."}`.
- `_require` treats `restart_preseal_<n>` as a non-stage key.

**The test world** `tests/brain/ab_world.py` (as `aa_world`):
- `World(tmp_path, monkeypatch, s=None)` chdirs into tmp and uses `s = small(SPEC, archive_root=tmp/arch, boot_b=200, boot_chunk=100, n_sel=30, n_ver=40, truth_pairs=2_000, truth_chunk=1_000, cal_chunk=7, bench_reps=2, synth_reps=2, workers=2)`.
- **Fake ctx:**
  - `docs()` from synthetic summaries in tmp: AA with nine PASS blocks and 31 trained candidates; W pilot manifest keys; Y admission.
  - `base()` / `lv()` / `generate()` / `selftest()` are scripted:
    - `generate(lv)` returns a 40-row KC-pre set: (a) 30 rows over 7 X labels and 9 type sets, (b) 10 rows; 20 odours, 10 of them "new";
    - every declared count equals a world-local `ABSpec` replace, `decl_*` = the world's values;
    - `selftest()` returns ok.
  - `kc_measurer` / `oracle_measurer` are scripted fakes (`activity` returns medians by odour from a table; `oracle` returns `r_fixtures.fake_oracle` with testable by plan, written as cache files under `results/ab/cache/r_oracle`).
  - `learn_measurer` = the REAL WMeasurer over `ABCache` with `tests.brain.w_world.FakePool(Model())` (`Model.effects` per pair sets the learning size).
  - The AA differential pieces: a synthetic AA learn cache written by `aa_runner.units` + WMeasurer into `results/aa/cache` (a `ReadCache` reads it), with records / estimate files computed by `aa_estimate` from the same raw.
- `summary_git` / `git_state` are monkeypatched clean, and a passing tests log is written.
- `chain(last)` runs the stages up to `last` and asserts PASS.

### Task 8: `ab_runner` (B) — `cal_gate` (0e), `futility` (0f), `kc_input` (3), `set` (4); the pooled checkpointing cell executor

**Parallel group:** P4. **Depends on:** T7, T6b.

**Files:** Modify `flymon/brain/ab_runner.py`, `flymon/brain/ab_spec.py` (add `"futility"` to `stages` after `cal_gate` with label `"0f"`, and to `sealed_stages` by construction — `TODO(AB.9.3: the order label)`). Create `tests/brain/test_ab_runner_b.py`.

**Spec:**
- AB.5 6752–6765 (0e, the synthetic validation, checkpoints, cost);
- AB.7 0e 6809 (the reservation, `STOP_CALIBRATION`, `STOP_BUDGET`〈0e 전 / 0e〉);
- AB.9.3 (0f);
- AB.7 3 6810 (KC + repro, `STOP_KC_REPRO`);
- AB.7 4 6811 (set, `STOP_SET_MISMATCH`〈4〉, c);
- AB.3 6690–6691.

**The cell executor (Reading 9) — key code:**

```python
def _cal_job(kind: str, args: tuple, path: str, key: str, plist) -> dict:
    """One calibration job in a spawn worker (module level: pickled by name). Resumes from its checkpoint when the key
    matches; checkpoints after every chunk (ab_store atomic write)."""
    p = Path(path)
    old = json.loads(p.read_text()) if p.exists() else None
    if old is not None and old.get("key") == key and old.get("final"):
        return old["value"]
    t = time.perf_counter()
    if kind == "truth":
        v = ab_estimate.truth(*args)
        ab_store.write_json(path, dict(key=key, final=True, value=v, wall_s=time.perf_counter() - t), plist)
        return v
    if kind == "synth":
        v = _synth_run(*args)
        ab_store.write_json(path, dict(key=key, final=True, value=v, wall_s=time.perf_counter() - t), plist)
        return v
    resume = old["value"] if old is not None and old.get("key") == key else None

    def save(pay):
        ab_store.write_json(path, dict(key=key, final=pay["done"] >= pay["n"], value=pay,
                                       wall_s=time.perf_counter() - t), plist)
    return ab_estimate.cal_run(*args, resume=resume, on_chunk=save)
```

- `cal_run`'s positional args stop at `n`. For "ver" jobs the runner passes `only` by keyword: the executor builds `functools.partial`-free calls — `args` holds (st, tag, phase, ci, tru, s, n, None, None, B, only), and `_cal_job` calls `ab_estimate.cal_run(*args[:7], resume=…, on_chunk=…, B=args[9], only=args[10])` for that arity.
- `CalExec(runner, stage, root)` has `__call__(kind, args)` and `many(jobs)`. `many` runs the jobs in `cf.ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=workers, mp_context=spawn)` with the thread env vars set to 1.
- Each job's path is `results/ab/cal/<tag>/<kind>_<ci>.json`. Its key is the sha256 of canonical(kind, args without `s`, `seal_now(s)["key"]`).
- As each future completes, `many` calls the stage ticker. If the measured core (ledger + this stage's progress seconds) > `core_cap_h`, it cancels the pending futures, shuts the pool down and raises `_BudgetStop`. The checkpoints written so far stay.

**`stage_cal_gate`** (0e):
1. `_require` (sealed stage: the seal check first).
2. Reservation:
   - est = Σ_tags 24 × (n_sel + n_ver) × `bench.max_s` / workers, plus the synthetic validation (5 configs × 4 cells × synth_reps × 3 × `bench.max_s` / workers), plus the futility estimate (`TODO(AB.9.3)`);
   - plus the remaining core estimate, by Reading 18's priors: KC, smoke = `prior_fixed_h` share, oracle 184, screen 40, 7b at k 26, learn 26, verdict.
   - If `not core_ok(core_h(doc), est_total)` → `budget_stop("0e 전", text, "2세대 측정 없음", counts)`.
3. `out = ab_estimate.calibrate_many([(rep_structure(sizes), tag) for tag, sizes in s.rep_structures], s, CalExec(...))`. `_BudgetStop` → `budget_stop("0e", spent_text, "2세대 측정 없음", …)`.
4. Failures = [(tag, stat) with `alpha[stat]["P"] is None`]. If there are any: build `calibration_stop` for the first one (in rep_structures order, then STATS order). Its phase is "sel" when `chosen[stat]["P"] is None` (the grid end, with the worst cell's x and CP at α 0.0001), else "ver" (`verified[stat]["P"]["worst"]`). All failures go into `reasons`.
5. The synthetic validation runs only when g 7's `pass_ok` holds (Reading 10). 20 jobs `("synth", (st_g7, config, ci, eff, alpha_g7, s))`, where `_synth_run` loops `synth_reps` × `ab_estimate.synth_rep` on stream `("synth", config, ci)` and returns label counts. The record holds P(PASS) and P(FAIL) per config × cell, labelled "기록 — 방법 · 수치를 바꾸지 않는다".
6. The block holds, per structure: α (P per stat; F of D or the flag "FAIL 쪽 미도달 위험"), the selection counts and CP per cell and level, the verification counts at the chosen α, the per-method single misses, the stream tags, the truths, and the synthetic validation (or null + reason). The STOP sentence or PASS follows.

**`stage_futility`** (0f) — `TODO(AB.9.3)` for every constant:
1. `_require` (sealed), then a reservation of the futility estimate + the remaining core (the 0e form). `STOP_BUDGET`〈0f 전〉 / 〈0f〉 when AB.9.3 names them (`TODO(AB.9.3: the budget guards)`).
2. Levels from the `cal_gate` block, on the structure(s) AB.9.3 names.
3. `ab_estimate.futility(…)` through `CalExec(root="results/ab/cal/futility")`. Resumable.
4. P(PASS) < threshold → `STOP_FUTILE` (a new label in `ab_rules`: `TODO(AB.9.3: the sentence)`; Task 3's file gains it in this task, with a test). Then the record-only what-if rows.
5. Otherwise PASS, carrying P(PASS) and its CP bounds.

**`stage_kc_input`** (3, the first Gen-2 measurement):

```python
    def stage_kc_input(self) -> dict:
        doc = self._require("kc_input")
        s, ctx = self.s, self.ctx
        gen = json.loads(Path(doc["generate"]["detail_path"]).read_text())
        ids = list(gen["new_odours"]) + list(s.kc_repro)
        od = ctx["odour_inputs"](ids)
        m = ctx["kc_measurer"](self.pool)
        tick = self._ticker("kc_input")
        try:
            act = {e: m.activity(od, edit, V_SPEC.strength, V_SPEC.kc_seeds(), "kc_input")
                   for e, edit in (("none", V_SPEC.no_edit), ("lever", V_SPEC.lever_edit))}
        finally:
            tick()
        per = {e: e_rules.odour_activity({o: act[e][o]["frac"] for o in act[e]}) for e in act}
        diffs = ab_rules.kc_repro_diffs(list(s.kc_repro), ctx["kc_v"](), per)
        new = {e: {o: per[e][o] for o in gen["new_odours"]} for e in per}
        p = ab_store.write_json(self._detail("kc_input"), dict(activity=act, per_odour=per), self.plist)
        body = (ab_rules.kc_repro_stop(list(s.kc_repro), diffs, self._counts(doc)) if diffs
                else dict(outcome=ab_rules.PASS, reasons=[]))
        return self._write("kc_input", dict(body, new_per_odour=new, repro=dict(odours=list(s.kc_repro), diffs=diffs),
                                            seeds=list(V_SPEC.kc_seeds()), detail_path=self._detail("kc_input"),
                                            detail_sha256=sha256_file(p),
                                            note="AB.7 3: KC 값은 냄새의 성질이며 L_V 판정 결과가 아니다."))
```

- `e_rules` is `flymon.agent.e_rules`.
- Edits come from `V_SPEC.no_edit` and `V_SPEC.lever_edit`.
- `od` holds 75 odours (71 + 4). The 4 repro odours are re-measured in AB's empty cache.

**`stage_set`** (4):
- `kc = ab_pairs.kc_merge(kc_v, doc["kc_input"]["new_per_odour"])`.
- Read the KC-pre rows: regenerate them via `ctx["generate"](lv)["rows"]` and check that `keys` equal the generate block's keys.
- `rows = ab_pairs.ab_set(pre, kc)`.
- Second generation: rows2 = the same calls again. `[row_key(r) for r in rows] != …rows2` → `STOP_SET_MISMATCH`〈4〉.
- Block: `ab_pairs.set_record(rows, kc)`, n, c range, both group tables, the dropped odours per engine.
- The `candidates` ledger is created here: every row `untouched` with (key, c, axis) — Reading 17.

### Task 9: `ab_runner` (C) — `smoke` (5), `oracle` (6), `screen` (7), `calibrate` (7b)

**Parallel group:** P4. **Depends on:** T8.

**Files:** Modify `flymon/brain/ab_runner.py`. Create `tests/brain/test_ab_runner_c.py`.

**Spec:**
- AB.7 5 6812 (smoke + the reservation with 7b);
- AB.7 6 6813–6814 (oracle, counts only, `oracled`, `STOP_MACHINE`〈6〉, yield ①);
- AB.7 7 6815–6817 (screen, `screened`, the machine checks of AA.7 4, the S1 manifest, yield ②, 7b + `STOP_BUDGET`〈7b 전〉 + `STOP_COVERAGE`);
- AB.2 6645–6646 (oracle path, filters);
- AB.9 6892 (consumption from 6).

**`stage_smoke`** (Readings 18, 20):
1. `row = ctx["smoke_row"]()`; `ws = ab_w_spec(s)` with `smoke=True` for `oracle_seeds()`. Use `s.smoke_oracle_seeds()` directly.
2. Jobs on the smoke cache:
   - one oracle job: `ctx["oracle_measurer"](pool, True).oracle([row], V_SPEC.cond("L"), "smoke", s.smoke_oracle_seeds())`;
   - one naive pre job and one R / N / RN learn set: `aa_runner.units([row], "smoke", K, 1, V_SPEC.lever_edit, ws=ws)` and the same with `brains=("naive",)`, through `ctx["learn_measurer"](pool, True)`.
3. `problems` (any → `INVALID`, a pipeline defect before the judged measurements — fix via `restart_preseal` if before 3, else the defect procedure):
   - `w_records.machine_reasons(learn, [0], aa_runner.declared(lever, seeds), naive=…)`;
   - RN1 = R1;
   - the oracle lever (edit / CSC / edges);
   - the oracle's z = `W_SPEC.z_v()`; the learn z (W reuse z_V) = `ctx["w_z"]()`;
   - every seed in the smoke blocks (`s.seed_blocks[4:7]`).
4. Costs: the oracle job wall, the naive job wall and the largest learn job wall. Estimates by Reading 18. The verdict estimate times one synthetic `set_limits` + `verdict` at k 26.
5. Reservation (AB.7 5): `core_ok(core_h + this stage, oracle + screen(40) + 7b(k 26, 0e's measured s/rep) + learn(26) + noplast + verdict)`. Else `STOP_BUDGET`〈5〉 ("2세대 판정 측정 없음").
6. Block: problems, costs, estimates_h, budget, seeds, manifest (archived with the raw).

**`stage_oracle`** (6):
1. Rows = the order-4 set regenerated (`ctx["generate"]` + `kc_merge` + `ab_set`, keys checked against block `set`), attached with odours.
2. `_cands_set("oracle", keys, "oracled")` before the batch (Reading 17).
3. `got = ctx["oracle_measurer"](pool, False).oracle(rows, V_SPEC.cond("L"), "screen", s.oracle_seeds())`.
4. `rec = [y_runner.Runner._record(r, g["key"], g["result"], W_SPEC.z_v()) for r, g in zip(rows, got)]`.
5. Machine checks: `ab_rules.oracle_machine_reasons(got, rows, seeds, s.n_oracle_seeds)` + the lever check (`rec["failure"]`). Any → `machine_stop("오라클", …, "6")`.
6. `lenient = [r for r in rec if y_rules.passes(r, Y_SPEC)["y_lenient"]]`, with its counts and groups (X labels via `merge_groups`, type sets via `ab_pairs.ts_key`).
7. Detail `results/ab/oracle.json` = {pairs: rec, manifest, seeds, z_V}. **The block holds counts only**: by axis n, testable, N_len; the lenient pairs' X-label group count and type-set group count.
8. Yield ①: N_len < 8 or X-label groups < 5 → `few_pairs_oracle`.

**`stage_screen`** (7):
1. The lenient items come from `results/ab/oracle.json` (sha-checked against block `oracle`) and are read for (key, c, axis) only. Rows by c.
2. `_cands_set("screen", keys, "screened")` before the batch.
3. Units: `aa_runner.units(rows, "main", K, F, lever, brains=("naive",), ws=ab_w_spec(s))`; identical inputs are measured once (AA form); block `"screen"`.
4. Machine checks: `aa_rules.screen_machine_reasons(units, counts, want_seeds, N_len × F, K)` + the raw digest (`aa_runner.raw_digest`). Any → `machine_stop("순진 거름", …, "7")`.
5. Per pair, `y_rules.final_filter(pre, z_W, Y_SPEC)` (z_W = W reuse z_V; Reading 5) — **the only Gen-2 `final_filter` call**.
6. The S1 manifest (block): per pair (key, c, axis, x_label = `y_rules.x_odour(key)`, tset = `ts_key(row)`, naive_d, L_A, L_P, passed); k; both group counts; sizes per group.
7. Yield ②: k < 8, X groups < 5 or type-set groups < 5 → `few_pairs_screen` (with `ab_rules.reason_counts`).
8. Re-read the raw right after the block (Reading 25).

**`stage_calibrate`** (7b):
1. `_require` (sealed). Re-read the screen raw by manifest and check its digest (else `STOP_MACHINE`〈7〉).
2. Reservation: `core_ok(core_h, 7b est + learn(k) + noplast + verdict)`. Else `STOP_BUDGET`〈7b 전〉 ("학습 0").
3. `st = ab_estimate.structure([S1 keys in c order], [S1 tsets])`; `cal = ab_estimate.calibrate(st, "S1", s, CalExec(...))`.
4. Any `alpha[stat]["P"] is None` → `coverage_stop(k, X sizes, T sizes, stat, phase, cell, cp, counts)`.
5. Else PASS with `alpha` = {D: α_P^D, Dfin: α_P^F, R: α_P^R, F: α_F or None}, the per-cell counts / CP, and the flag "FAIL 쪽 미도달" when F is None. **No result value enters**: the structure tuple only.

**Tests** `tests/brain/test_ab_runner_c.py`:
1. `test_smoke_pass_costs_and_seeds_in_smoke_blocks`.
2. `test_smoke_budget_stop`.
3. `test_smoke_pre_mismatch_is_a_problem` (Model `share_state`).
4. `test_smoke_z_checks` (oracle z_v, learn z_V).
5. `test_oracle_block_has_counts_only` — no `d_pre` / `L_A` in the block; `results/ab/oracle.json` has them; candidates `oracled`.
6. `test_oracle_yield_one_stop` (N_len 7; and N_len 9 with 4 groups).
7. `test_oracle_machine_stop` (a NaN report).
8. `test_screen_s1_manifest_candidates_and_seeds_use_c` — the seeds = 88_100_000 + c × 4 000 + f × 100 + k with c = the AB candidate number. Also the mutants "c 대신 S1 안의 번호", "주 세트 시드".
9. `test_screen_only_lenient_pairs`.
10. `test_screen_yield_two_stop` (k 7; k 9 with 4 type-set groups — the mutant "k_min · g_min 생략").
11. `test_screen_rereads_raw` / `test_screen_resumes_from_cache`.
12. `test_calibrate_structure_only_and_levels_block` — `calibrate` called with the structure tuple (a spy).
13. `test_calibrate_stop_coverage`.
14. `test_calibrate_reservation_stop`.
15. `test_calibrate_fail_side_flag`.

- [ ] Steps: tests → fail → code → `t9.log` exit 0 (< 8 min) → commit `git commit -m "ab: ab_runner C — 5 smoke (+ reservation with 7b), 6 oracle (counts only, oracled, yield ①), 7 screen (S1 manifest, screened, yield ②), 7b calibrate (STOP_COVERAGE)"`.

**Acceptance:** `t9.log` `exit 0`. Exactly one function calls `final_filter` on Gen-2 data (`stage_screen`). Task 11 checks it by AST.

### Task 10: `ab_runner` (D) — `learn` (8), `verdict` (9), `records` (10), `defect` / `reseal` / `recompute`

**Parallel group:** P4. **Depends on:** T9.

**Files:** Modify `flymon/brain/ab_runner.py`. Create `tests/brain/test_ab_runner_d.py`.

**Spec:**
- AB.7 8 6818–6820 (learn, reservation, measured cap, noplast, machine checks, `training` → `trained`, resume);
- AB.7 9 6821 (verdict, seal, repeat bit-equal);
- AB.7 10 6822, AB.4 기록 6720, AB.6 6772–6773 (records);
- AB.7 0d 6807 + AA.7 3 (defect procedure);
- AB.8 PASS / FAIL / UNDECIDED.

**`stage_learn`** (8):
1. Units = S1 (screen block `passed`, c order) × F × (R, N, RN), from `aa_runner.units(…, "main", …, ws=ab_w_spec(s))`, block `"learn"`. Then noplast (Reading 21): `brains=("R",)`, `plastic=False`, `brain="noplast"`, F 2, block `"noplast"`, S1's first 2 pairs.
2. Reservation before the start: `core_ok(core_h, remaining learn + noplast + verdict)`. Else `STOP_BUDGET`〈8 전〉 ("학습 0").
3. The round check `check(a, n)`: tick; the measured cap (`spent_ok`), else `_BudgetStop` → `budget_stop("8", spent_text, f"학습 {n}쌍 완료, 부분 판정 없음")`; then `_cands_set("learn", round pairs, "training")`.
4. After the measurer returns: for each pair, every planned unit key is in the cache (`wm.cache.get`) and its manifest entry carries a sha256 → `_cands_set(…, "trained")`. Never `trained` at round start (the mutant).
5. Machine checks (→ `machine_stop("학습", …, "8")`):
   - unit count / duplicates;
   - RN1 = R1;
   - learn pre = screen pre, bit for bit (the screen raw re-read);
   - `w_records.machine_reasons`, `w_verdict.data_reasons`;
   - no NaN fly;
   - noplast R1 = pre (bit).
6. The archive copy (AB.7 8 6819) is never overwritten; `ab_store.archive` refuses a differing file set. A same-sha repeat is a no-op.
7. The block holds the manifest only: no statistic, no `naive_d`, no `gate_stats`.

**`stage_verdict`** (9):
1. `_require` (sealed; a seal mismatch → exit 7 unless a reseal exists).
2. Load the learn raw by manifest (sha-checked, cache hits only). `per = {k: ab_estimate.pair_stats(data[k], z_W, s)}` — a NaN → `machine_stop` (verdict row 1).
3. `mech = {k: w_verdict.mech_fractions(data[k])}`. machine = the learn block's reasons (already PASS) + the noplast result.
4. `alpha` comes **from block `calibrate`** (`_eff`): {D, Dfin, R, F}. The mutant "보정 수준 대신 95% 고정" is killed by `test_verdict_reads_7b_levels`.
5. `lim = ab_estimate.set_limits(per, keys, tsets, ab_estimate.levels_for(alpha), s)`; `v = ab_estimate.verdict(lim, machine, mech, alpha, s)`.
6. Compute everything a second time; not bit-equal → `INVALID` (exit 5, never committed; Reading 22).
7. The sentence:
   - **PASS:** `pass_sentence` with the unfavourable ends = for each gate the min (+) / max (−) of TS and CG on the Hedges scale for D and D_fin, the z scale for R; `fly_counts`; φ medians from `aa_estimate.floors`; df = `cg_parts(...)["df"]` of D.
   - **FAIL:** the first failing gate in GATES order, with `past_zero`.
   - **UNDECIDED:** the causes.
   - **STOP_PROTOCOL:** the first bad pair.
8. The block holds: per gate × stat × method the point and limits (raw and Hedges), the levels, k / k_fin / groups, fly counts, the label, causes, the sentence, `repeat_equal`, `seal_key`, `calibrate_block`. The detail file holds the per-pair arrays.

**`stage_records`** (10, the records ledger with its 4 h cap; nulls + "records 상한", never a STOP):
- the uncorrected scale side by side;
- `fly_counts`, `naive_record`, `record_cis` (fly / pair);
- `min_of_gates(point, D _reps)`;
- the per-pair records: `aa_estimate.pair_record` + R + floors + mech shares;
- DL / HK (`aa_estimate.dl_hk` on the D pair estimates);
- the unwinsorized means;
- the `compare_table` per gate;
- the φ quantiles (`aa_estimate.phi_quantiles`);
- the floor sensitivity: `floor_subset` → if k ≥ 2 and both group counts ≥ 2, `calibrate(st_sub, "sens", s, CalExec(stage="records"))` + `set_limits` on stream `("sens_floor", "sens")`, else null + reason;
- the 0e / 7b per-cell misses and single-method misses copied from their blocks.

**Defect procedure:** copy `aa_runner.defect` / `reseal` / `recompute` with AB names. `recompute_stages = ("set", "oracle", "screen", "calibrate", "learn", "verdict", "records")` (Reading 16); recompute uses the measurers in cache-only mode (a miss → refuse, exit 2).

**Tests** `tests/brain/test_ab_runner_d.py`:
1. `test_learn_s1_only_c_order_states_training_then_trained` (the mutants "관대 탈락쌍 학습", "trained at round start").
2. `test_learn_pre_equals_screen_pre_and_rn1` (parametrized cases).
3. `test_learn_noplast_mismatch_stop_machine` (the mutant "noplast 삭제").
4. `test_learn_reservation_and_measured_cap`.
5. `test_learn_resumes_and_resumed_units_bit_equal`.
6. `test_learn_block_has_no_statistic`.
7. `test_verdict_pass_world` (large effects) / `test_verdict_fail_world` (reversed effects) / `test_verdict_undecided_world` (small k effect).
8. `test_verdict_reads_7b_levels`.
9. `test_s1_never_changes_with_learning_values` (the mutants "S1을 학습 값으로", "학습 뒤 순진 값 재계산").
10. `test_verdict_protocol_stop` (mech 0.74).
11. `test_verdict_repeat_mismatch_invalid`.
12. `test_verdict_seal_mismatch_exit7`.
13. `test_records_full_and_cap_nulls`.
14. `test_records_floor_sensitivity_null_small_k`.
15. `test_defect_reseal_recompute_verdict_cache_only`.
16. `test_recompute_refuses_measurement`.

- [ ] Steps: tests → fail → code → `t10.log` exit 0 (< 8 min) → commit `git commit -m "ab: ab_runner D — 8 learn (training → trained, noplast STOP_MACHINE, resume), 9 verdict (7b levels, repeat bit-equal, AB.8 sentences), 10 records, defect / reseal / recompute"`.

**Acceptance:** `t10.log` `exit 0`. The full world chain `stage0 … records` runs PASS in the tests (`World.chain("records")`).

## Operator section (controller runs; never an implementer)

All runs happen in the **main checkout** `/Users/jeonsehyeon/orca/workspaces/fruit-fly/guillemot`, after the whole-branch review.
- Each block is committed before the next stage starts (AB.7 머리 6776).
- Every run goes in the background (Bash `run_in_background: true`). On notification, read only the exit code, the `stage … outcome` line and the sentence line (`tail -3` of the run log).
- **Never open** the block details, `results/ab/oracle.json`, the screen / learn raw or the `cal/` checkpoints.
- Update the ledger `/private/tmp/claude-503/ab-sdd-ledger.md` after every block.

**§0 Setup (before Task 1).**

```bash
git -C /Users/jeonsehyeon/orca/workspaces/fruit-fly/guillemot worktree add /Users/jeonsehyeon/orca/workspaces/fruit-fly/guillemot-ab -b ab-impl HEAD
cd /Users/jeonsehyeon/orca/workspaces/fruit-fly/guillemot-ab && ln -s /Users/jeonsehyeon/orca/workspaces/fruit-fly/guillemot/.venv .venv && ln -s /Users/jeonsehyeon/orca/workspaces/fruit-fly/guillemot/data/malecns.npz data/malecns.npz && for d in v w x y z aa; do ln -s /Users/jeonsehyeon/orca/workspaces/fruit-fly/guillemot/results/$d results/$d; done
```

```bash
cd /Users/jeonsehyeon/orca/workspaces/fruit-fly/guillemot && mkdir -p results/ab && .venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/brain/test_ab_*.py tests/brain/test_p_spec.py tests/brain/test_aa_*.py tests/brain/test_z_*.py tests/brain/test_y_*.py tests/brain/test_x_*.py tests/brain/test_w_*.py -o addopts="" -q -p no:cacheprovider > results/ab/tests_0a.log 2>&1; echo "exit $?" >> results/ab/tests_0a.log
```

Read `tail -2 results/ab/tests_0a.log`. Stage0 is `INVALID` unless the last line is `exit 0` and the line before it is `N passed in Xs`, with no `failed` / `error` summary. On failure: fix in the worktree, re-review, re-merge, rerun §2. If any AB block exists, use `restart_preseal` first (§4).

**§3 Stages.** Each stage is one command: `.venv/bin/python scripts/run_ab.py --stage <stage> [--workers 16] > /private/tmp/claude-503/ab-run/<stage>.log 2>&1; echo "exit $?" >> …`.
- After exit 0 or exit 3 (a STOP), commit only the summary: `git add results/summary/ab_learning.json && git commit -m "<message>"` (no trailers).
- Then archive: `.venv/bin/python scripts/run_ab.py --stage archive --name <stage>`.
- Then push: `gh auth switch --user lyutvs && git push origin HEAD:main` (memory: push verified commits and continue).

**Exit codes and the rerun / INVALID rules** (Reading 29; AB.7 0d 6806–6808; AA.7 3):
- **0** — commit, archive, push, next stage.
- **3 (STOP)** — commit the block (its archive copy was made before the sentence), archive, push, then go to **§6 AB.10**. A STOP'd stage is never rerun. The cases:
  - `STOP_REUSE` 0b, `STOP_SET_MISMATCH` 0c / 4;
  - `STOP_CALIBRATION` 0e, `STOP_FUTILE` 0f;
  - `STOP_BUDGET` 0e 전 / 0e / 5 / 7b 전 / 8 전 / 8;
  - `STOP_KC_REPRO` 3, `STOP_MACHINE` 6 / 7 / 8, `STOP_FEW_PAIRS` 6 / 7, `STOP_COVERAGE` 7b;
  - verdict labels FAIL / UNDECIDED / STOP_PROTOCOL / STOP_MACHINE are exit 3 too.
- **5 (INVALID)** — do not commit. `git checkout results/summary/ab_learning.json`, then:
  - **before `kc_input` exists** (an INVALID `stage0`, or a code fault found in `generate` / `seal_code` / `cal_gate` / `futility`): §4 `restart_preseal`;
  - **after the first measurement**: §5, the defect procedure.
- **6 (env mismatch)** — do not commit. Record it in the ledger and stop for the user.
- **7 (seal mismatch)** — do not commit. If no defect block explains it, stop for the user.
- **2 (refusal)** — read the stderr line, fix the cause (chain order, dirty file), rerun. Nothing was written.
- **A killed run** (tool limit, crash, reboot) is not a STOP (AB.7 8 6820). Rerun the same command: cache / checkpoints resume, and the spend stays in `results/ab/progress/`.
- **Never** delete or edit `results/ab/cache/` or `results/ab/cal/`. Wrong raw itself is `STOP_MACHINE`, the user's case.

**§4 restart_preseal** (before the first measurement; Reading 15):
1. Fix in the worktree (TDD), re-review, re-merge.
2. Write the note `/private/tmp/claude-503/ab-restart-<n>.json` = {symptom, clause, cause}.
3. `.venv/bin/python scripts/run_ab.py --stage restart_preseal --note <file>`. It is refused (exit 2) once `kc_input` exists or the cache has raw.
4. Commit `results(ab): restart_preseal <n> — <cause>; earlier blocks INVALID`, push.
5. Rerun §2, then from `stage0`. AB.10 discloses every restart.

**§5 Defect procedure** (after the first measurement; AA.7 3 form):
1. `--stage defect --note <json {symptom, clause, cause, affected}>` → commit `defect_<n>`.
2. Patch in the worktree with a regression test that fails on the old code; review; merge.
3. Rerun §2 (a new tests log).
4. `--stage reseal --name <n>` → commit.
5. `--stage recompute --name <stage>` for each affected stage, in order (cache hits only) → commit. The original is `INVALID`, the corrected block is primary, both are archived and disclosed.

A fix that needs a change of the AB text is an analysis-rule change: there is no primary result, and it goes to the user.

**§6 AB.10 checklist (order 11 — after `records`, or after any STOP / verdict label; standing approval "사용자 사전 승인(2026-10-06)에 따라 권장안 채택", but the AB.9 results → decisions are the user's):**
1. **Spec AB.10** — append after AB.9.2 / AB.9.3 at the end of appendix AB:
   - the outcome label and the verbatim AB.8 sentence;
   - every block's key numbers: bench, 0e α per structure + the synthetic validation, 0f P(PASS), KC drops, set n, N_len, k, groups, 7b α, learn, verdict table (4 gates × 3 statistics × 2 methods, Hedges and uncorrected), fly counts;
   - the records;
   - the AB.0 15 surprise checks ("AA와 다름" lines);
   - the candidate-state counts and the AB set status (AB.9 6892);
   - the budget (both ledgers);
   - every `restart_preseal` / defect;
   - this plan's Readings 1–30 and the Plan readings list below.
2. **Spec 5 status line (217)** — only for PASS / FAIL / UNDECIDED / `STOP_PROTOCOL` (AB.1 개정 6, 6636; 해석 17): append one line with the scope phrase after the old line, which stays as history. **No line for a pre-verdict STOP.**
3. **README ko / en** ledger rows, in the form of AA's rows at README 225 / 438:
   - (ko) `- **<판정 / STOP>(…, 2026-10-xx, 부록 AB.10 — <label>)**: …`;
   - (en) the matching row.

   Commit `docs(ab): AB.10 result — <label> …; README ko/en`, push.
4. **`/private/tmp/claude-503/p-latest.md`** — overwrite the full text (memory "p-latest file handoff") with the result, and:
   - for PASS: the M3 decision input (AB.9 다리);
   - for FAIL: the redesign decision input;
   - otherwise: the closure rule (AB.9 6898) — **no new judgement candidate for this lever** (해석 29).
5. **Memory:** update `~/.claude/projects/-Users-jeonsehyeon-orca-projects-fruit-fly/memory/flymon-appendix-ab.md` (the result, commits, next = user) and its `MEMORY.md` index line.
6. Stop for the user (AB.9 6899 "다음").

## Risks (for the controller before dispatching)

1. **`STOP_CALIBRATION`〈0e〉 is close to a coin flip** (plan-time probe, synthetic only).
   - At the grid end α 0.0001, cell (19) — the left-skewed X-label effect, SD 2 — gives a g 7 joint PASS-side miss of R ≈ 2.0 % (82 / 4 000 over two probes) and D ≈ 1.7 %. Selection needs x ≤ 103 / 5 000 (2.06 %), and verification needs ≤ 219 / 10 000.
   - g 5 is safe (0.4–0.7 %). g 6 is ≈ 1.1–1.4 %.
   - So 0e may stop the track before any Gen-2 measurement (AB.0 11 anticipates this; the closure rule follows) after the whole implementation is built.
   - The spec does not allow building stages 3–10 only after 0e: the env hash binds all `ab_*` code from 0a on.
   - The controller / user may want this known before dispatching (no spec change is implied by the plan).
2. **The AB.3 skip counts hold only as the declared-form tally (Reading 2).** In-order counting gives `in_set` 845 / `lv_odour` 1 276 against the declared 1 994 / 127. The rows are identical. If a reviewer reads AB.3 as in-order counts, 0c would end in `STOP_SET_MISMATCH`〈0c〉 by 해석 9. The spec's own sentence 6672 supports the declared form.
3. **The AB.9.3 futility gate** is not in the spec at `5e3eb7e`. Tasks 6b / 8 and the 0f Operator row carry `TODO(AB.9.3)` and must be filled from the committed text (Operator §0b). Its compute cost is unknown: it is a simulation at B 10 000 over the full verdict, possibly comparable to 0e's synthetic validation. Its budget guard is to be read from AB.9.3.
4. **Machine speed.** The 16-worker throughput is ≈ 10 effective cores (6P + 12E). 0e is then ≈ 2.5–3 h, not the spec's ≈ 2 h. The 0e reservation uses the single-process `bench` / workers (the spec's form), so it underestimates by ≈ 1.5×. That fits inside the × 2.0 margin, but the measured cap could matter at 7b / 8 if learn is ≥ 1.9× its estimate again.
5. **Runner code (Tasks 7–10) was not run at plan time.** It adapts `aa_runner`'s validated plumbing. Tasks 1–6 ran (85 passed incl. 22 store tests). The AA differential test was validated bit-equal on the real AA raw.
6. **The scratch skew numbers in AB.9.2** came from an SD 4 skew (Reading 27). The normative SD 2 cells behave differently. AB.10 should say so.
